```rust
#[tokio::test]
async fn reset_cancels_and_nothing_from_before_matches_after() {
    let a = pending("m", "a");
    let s = select(snapshot(vec![a.clone()]), "m/a");
    let port = FakePort::with(&[&a]);
    let mut exec = Executor::new();
    let old = say(&mut exec, &port, &s, "approve").await.hud.awaiting_display().unwrap();
    exec.shown(&port, old);
    exec.reset(&port);
    assert_eq!(port.cancelled(), ["act-0"], "unsent work is cancelled");
    // An utterance still being routed when voice is turned off.
    let (old_utterance, _) = exec.begin(&port, s.clone(), VoiceText::new("yes always"));
    exec.reset(&port);
    let routed = route(&s, &VoiceText::new("yes always"), None, &RouteOptions::default()).await;
    assert_eq!(exec.routed(old_utterance, routed), None, "a response from before the reset");
    let new = say(&mut exec, &port, &s, "yes always").await.hud.awaiting_display().unwrap();
    assert!(new > old, "proposal ids keep growing across a reset");
    assert_eq!(exec.shown(&port, old), None);
    assert_eq!(exec.confirm(&port, Some(old)), None);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn outcomes_are_reported_and_others_ignored() {
    let a = pending("m", "a");
    let s = select(snapshot(vec![a.clone()]), "m/a");
    let port = FakePort::with(&[&a]);
    for (outcome, view) in [
        (VoiceOutcome::Rejected { code: ErrorCode::RequestGone }, OutcomeView::Rejected { code: ErrorCode::RequestGone }),
        (VoiceOutcome::Uncertain, OutcomeView::Uncertain),
        (VoiceOutcome::NotSent { reason: NotSentReason::Changed }, OutcomeView::NotSent { reason: NotSentReason::Changed }),
    ] {
        let mut exec = Executor::new();
        say_shown(&mut exec, &port, &s, "approve").await;
        let id = exec.current_action().unwrap().to_owned();
        assert_eq!(exec.outcome("someone-else", outcome), None);
        let r = exec.outcome(&id, outcome).unwrap();
        assert!(matches!(r.hud, Hud::Done { outcome: o, .. } if o == view));
    }
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_lagged_stream_cancels_unsent_work_and_marks_fired_work_uncertain() {
    let a = pending("m", "a");
    let s = select(snapshot(vec![a.clone()]), "m/a");
    let port = FakePort::with(&[&a]);
    let mut exec = Executor::new();
    say_shown(&mut exec, &port, &s, "approve").await;
    let r = exec.outcomes_lost(&port).unwrap();
    assert!(matches!(r.hud, Hud::Done { outcome: OutcomeView::Cancelled, .. }));
    assert_eq!(port.cancelled(), ["act-0"]);

    say_shown(&mut exec, &port, &s, "approve").await;
    port.cancel_works.store(false, Ordering::SeqCst);
    let r = exec.outcomes_lost(&port).unwrap();
    assert!(matches!(r.hud, Hud::Done { outcome: OutcomeView::Uncertain, .. }));
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn read_summarize_and_select_change_nothing() {
    let a = pending("m", "a");
    let s = select(snapshot(vec![a.clone(), pending("m", "b")]), "m/a");
    let port = FakePort::with(&[&a]);
    let mut exec = Executor::new();
    let r = say(&mut exec, &port, &s, "read it").await;
    assert_eq!(r.hud, Hud::Speaking { text: VoiceText::new("Bash approval. npm test") });
    let r = say(&mut exec, &port, &s, "next").await;
    assert_eq!(r.select.as_deref(), Some("m/b"));
    assert!(port.enqueued().is_empty());
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn enqueue_refusals_are_classified() {
    let a = pending("m", "a");
    let s = select(snapshot(vec![a.clone()]), "m/a");
    let port = FakePort::with(&[&a]);
    *port.refuse.lock().unwrap() = Some(EnqueueError::AlreadyCounting);
    let r = say_shown(&mut Executor::new(), &port, &s, "approve").await;
    assert_eq!(r.hud, error(Refusal::AlreadyCounting));
}

#[test]
fn only_did_not_understand_shows_the_transcript() {
    let port = FakePort::with(&[]);
    let mut exec = Executor::new();
    let (n, _) = exec.begin(&port, snapshot(vec![]), VoiceText::new("frobnicate"));
    let r = exec.routed(n, Routed::Refused(Refusal::DidNotUnderstand)).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(r.hud, Hud::Error { refusal: Refusal::DidNotUnderstand, transcript: Some(VoiceText::new("frobnicate")) });
}

#[test]
fn debug_output_never_carries_voice_text() {
    let w = working("m", "w");
    let s = select(snapshot(vec![w.clone()]), "m/w");
    let port = FakePort::with(&[&w]);
    let mut exec = Executor::new();
    let (n, thinking) = exec.begin(&port, s, VoiceText::new("SECRET-T"));
    let send = Routed::Proposal(Proposal {
        step: Step::Send { card: "m/w".into(), text: VoiceText::new("SECRET-P") },
        origin: Origin::Model,
        provenance: Some(Provenance::Selected),
        off_screen: false,
        level: Level::Confirm,
    });
    let confirm = exec.routed(n, send).unwrap();
    for shown in [format!("{thinking:?}"), format!("{confirm:?}"), format!("{exec:?}")] {
        assert!(!shown.contains("SECRET"), "{shown}");
    }
    let id = confirm.hud.awaiting_display().unwrap();
    exec.shown(&port, id);
    let countdown = exec.confirm(&port, Some(id)).unwrap();
    assert!(!format!("{countdown:?} {:?}", port.enqueued()).contains("SECRET"));
}

#[test]
fn the_hud_json_shape_is_the_frontend_contract() {
    let target = acd_voice::hud::TargetView {
        card: "m/a".into(),
        machine: "minos".into(),
        agent: "Claude".into(),
        title: "Fix".into(),
        request: Some("bash approval".into()),
        off_screen: false,
    };
    let hud = Hud::WhichOne { candidates: vec![target], transcript: VoiceText::new("approve") };
    let v = serde_json::to_value(HudEvent { seq: 7, hud: &hud }).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(v["seq"], 7);
    assert_eq!(v["hud"]["state"], "which_one");
    assert_eq!(v["hud"]["candidates"][0]["request"], "bash approval");
    assert_eq!(v["hud"]["candidates"][0]["off_screen"], false);
    let v = serde_json::to_value(Hud::Done { proposal: None, outcome: OutcomeView::Rejected { code: ErrorCode::RequestGone } }).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(v["outcome"], serde_json::json!({"kind": "rejected", "code": "request_gone"}));
    let v = serde_json::to_value(Hud::Error { refusal: Refusal::DisplayFailed, transcript: None }).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(v, serde_json::json!({"state": "error", "refusal": "display_failed", "transcript": null}));
    assert!(!Hud::Hidden.fades() && !Hud::Hidden.wants_key());
    assert!(Hud::Speaking { text: VoiceText::new("x") }.fades());
}
```

`serde_json` is needed as a dev-dependency: add `serde_json.workspace = true` under `[dev-dependencies]` in `crates/acd-voice/Cargo.toml`. It is already a normal dependency, and integration tests can use it, but listing it makes that explicit.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the HUD view model**

Create `crates/acd-voice/src/hud.rs`:

```rust
//! What the HUD shows (spec §4.4). The app serializes it to the HUD
//! webview inside a `HudEvent`; `app/src/lib/hud.ts` mirrors these shapes.
use acd_protocol::{Effect, ErrorCode};
use serde::Serialize;

use crate::plan::NotSentReason;
use crate::refusal::Refusal;
use crate::router::target::Provenance;
use crate::safety::Level;
use crate::text::VoiceText;

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct TargetView {
    pub card: String,
    pub machine: String,
    /// "Claude", "Codex" or "agent".
    pub agent: String,
    pub title: String,
    /// The request's spoken type tag, e.g. "bash approval".
    pub request: Option<String>,
    /// Not on screen (machine filter, collapsed Done, hidden idle): the
    /// HUD stresses the machine (spec §5.2, D7).
    pub off_screen: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum ProposalAction {
    Answer,
    AnswerText,
    Send,
}

/// Target, action and full payload (spec §6.1): nothing here is cut.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct ProposalView {
    pub action: ProposalAction,
    pub target: TargetView,
    pub option: Option<String>,
    pub effect: Option<Effect>,
    /// The whole persistent rule of an `allow_always` option.
    pub rule: Option<String>,
    /// The request's command or question.
    pub question: Option<String>,
    /// The text an `answer_text` or `send` delivers.
    pub text: Option<VoiceText>,
    pub provenance: Option<Provenance>,
    pub level: Level,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
#[serde(tag = "kind", rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum OutcomeView {
    Delivered,
    Rejected { code: ErrorCode },
    Uncertain,
    Cancelled,
    NotSent { reason: NotSentReason },
    Selected,
}

// A short-lived UI value, built once per change and serialized; boxing the
// proposal would buy nothing.
#[allow(clippy::large_enum_variant)]
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
#[serde(tag = "state", rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum Hud {
    Hidden,
    /// Shown by the audio steps; step 1 has no microphone.
    Listening,
    Thinking { transcript: VoiceText },
    /// Waits for Enter, a click or "confirm" on this `proposal_id`, which
    /// the HUD must first acknowledge as shown (D22).
    Confirm { proposal: ProposalView, proposal_id: u64 },
    WhichOne { candidates: Vec<TargetView>, transcript: VoiceText },
    /// `action_id` is `None` until the HUD acknowledged `proposal_id` and
    /// the 2 s countdown started (D22).
    Countdown { proposal: ProposalView, proposal_id: u64, action_id: Option<String> },
    /// Fired; the result follows as `Done` whenever it arrives.
    Sending { proposal: ProposalView, too_late_to_cancel: bool },
    Done { proposal: Option<ProposalView>, outcome: OutcomeView },
    Error { refusal: Refusal, transcript: Option<VoiceText> },
    /// Step 1 shows the text; TTS comes in delivery step 4.
    Speaking { text: VoiceText },
}

impl Hud {
    /// The HUD takes key focus only while it waits for input (spec §4.4).
    pub fn wants_key(&self) -> bool {
        matches!(self, Hud::Confirm { .. } | Hud::WhichOne { .. } | Hud::Countdown { .. })
    }

    /// It fades on its own: 1.5 s after a step fires or ends, or after
    /// speech. A later outcome is shown again as `Done`.
    pub fn fades(&self) -> bool {
        matches!(self, Hud::Sending { .. } | Hud::Done { .. } | Hud::Error { .. } | Hud::Speaking { .. })
    }

    /// The proposal the HUD must acknowledge as shown (D22).
    pub fn awaiting_display(&self) -> Option<u64> {
        match self {
            Hud::Confirm { proposal_id, .. } | Hud::Countdown { proposal_id, action_id: None, .. } => Some(*proposal_id),
            _ => None,
        }
    }
}

/// The event payload. `seq` grows with every publish, so the HUD can drop
/// a state that arrives after a newer one.
#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct HudEvent<'a> {
    pub seq: u64,
    pub hud: &'a Hud,
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the executor**

Create `crates/acd-voice/src/plan.rs`:

```rust
//! One voice step, from proposal to outcome (spec §5.6). The app supplies
//! the inbox through `InboxPort`, so acd-voice stays independent of
//! acd-client.
use acd_protocol::{Effect, ErrorCode};
use serde::Serialize;

use crate::fast::{self, Locale, Reply};
use crate::hud::{Hud, OutcomeView, ProposalAction, ProposalView, TargetView};
use crate::refusal::Refusal;
use crate::router::prompt::{LABEL_MAX, TITLE_MAX};
use crate::router::{self, Choice, Proposal, Routed, Step};
use crate::safety::Level;
use crate::snapshot::{CardSnap, ConnStamp, Snapshot, Visibility};
use crate::speech;
use crate::text::{VoiceText, clean};

/// Why an action ended without being sent (D1).
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum NotSentReason {
    Offline,
    NotSupported,
    AgentGone,
    AnsweredElsewhere,
    Changed,
    AgentBusy,
    Hidden,
}

/// acd-client's action outcomes in acd-voice's terms (spec §5.6, D1–D3).
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum VoiceOutcome {
    Fired,
    Delivered,
    Rejected { code: ErrorCode },
    Uncertain,
    Cancelled,
    NotSent { reason: NotSentReason },
}

/// What a step was planned on (spec §5.6): the connection, the agent, and
/// its pending request (`None`: there was none). acd-client checks all of
/// it against the live machine under the lock that records the action.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Guard {
    pub conn: ConnStamp,
    pub agent_id: String,
    pub request: Option<(String, u64)>,
}

/// One client operation with its guard.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Enqueue {
    Pick { card: String, option_id: String, guard: Guard },
    AnswerText { card: String, text: VoiceText, guard: Guard },
    Reply { card: String, text: VoiceText, guard: Guard },
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, thiserror::Error)]
pub enum EnqueueError {
    #[error("the request changed")]
    Stale,
    #[error("the request cannot be answered here")]
    NotAnswerable,
    #[error("a countdown is already running for this card")]
    AlreadyCounting,
    #[error("the text was refused")]
    BadText,
    #[error("replies are not possible for this agent now")]
    CannotReply,
    #[error("the connection changed")]
    ConnectionChanged,
    #[error("unknown card")]
    UnknownCard,
}

/// The inbox operations voice uses; acd-voice-client implements it over
/// acd-client. `enqueue` is the authoritative live check.
pub trait InboxPort: Send + Sync {
    /// Starts the normal 2 s countdown if the guard still holds; returns
    /// the action id.
    fn enqueue(&self, op: Enqueue) -> Result<String, EnqueueError>;
    /// Cancels by action id; false once the countdown has ended.
    fn cancel(&self, action_id: &str) -> bool;
}

/// What the app shows, and a card to select in the panel.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Reaction {
    pub hud: Hud,
    pub select: Option<String>,
}

fn show(hud: Hud) -> Reaction {
    Reaction { hud, select: None }
}

/// What the trigger did to a running voice countdown (D15).
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
enum Prior {
    #[default]
    Nothing,
    Cancelled,
    TooLate,
}

#[derive(Debug, Default)]
enum State {
    #[default]
    Idle,
    Thinking,
    /// A countdown step on its way to the screen; nothing is enqueued until
    /// the HUD acknowledges proposal `id` (D22).
    Presenting { proposal: Proposal, id: u64 },
    Confirm { proposal: Proposal, id: u64, shown: bool },
    WhichOne(Choice),
    Counting { proposal: Proposal, id: u64, action_id: String },
    Sending { proposal: Proposal, action_id: String },
    Shown,
}

#[derive(Debug, Default)]
pub struct Executor {
    /// Grows forever, also across `reset` (B4).
    utterance: u64,
    /// Grows forever, also across `reset` (B4).
    proposals: u64,
    state: State,
    snapshot: Option<Snapshot>,
    transcript: Option<VoiceText>,
    prior: Prior,
}

impl Executor {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self::default()
    }

    /// The voice action whose outcomes matter now.
    pub fn current_action(&self) -> Option<&str> {
        match &self.state {
            State::Counting { action_id, .. } | State::Sending { action_id, .. } => Some(action_id),
            _ => None,
        }
    }

    /// A new utterance (the trigger, or the typed command). A running voice
    /// countdown is cancelled first (spec §4.4); the snapshot freezes.
    pub fn begin(&mut self, port: &dyn InboxPort, snapshot: Snapshot, transcript: VoiceText) -> (u64, Reaction) {
        self.prior = match &self.state {
            State::Counting { action_id, .. } if port.cancel(action_id) => Prior::Cancelled,
            State::Counting { .. } | State::Sending { .. } => Prior::TooLate,
            _ => Prior::Nothing,
        };
        self.utterance += 1;
        self.state = State::Thinking;
        self.snapshot = Some(snapshot);
        self.transcript = Some(transcript.clone());
        (self.utterance, show(Hud::Thinking { transcript }))
    }

    /// The router's result for utterance `n`; stale results are dropped.
    pub fn routed(&mut self, n: u64, routed: Routed) -> Option<Reaction> {
        if n != self.utterance || !matches!(self.state, State::Thinking) {
            return None;
        }
        Some(self.take(routed))
    }

    /// While the HUD waits for input, an utterance answers it (D21):
    /// "confirm" confirms a shown proposal, an ordinal or number chooses,
    /// "cancel" cancels. Anything else leaves the waiting step as it is.
    /// `None` when nothing waits; the utterance is then a new step.
    pub fn reply(&mut self, port: &dyn InboxPort, transcript: &VoiceText, locale: Locale) -> Option<Reaction> {
        let which_one = match self.state {
            State::Confirm { .. } => false,
            State::WhichOne(_) => true,
            _ => return None,
        };
        let reaction = match fast::parse_reply(transcript.expose(), locale) {
            Some(Reply::Confirm) if !which_one => self.confirm(port, None),
            Some(Reply::Choose(i)) if which_one => self.choose(i),
            Some(Reply::Cancel) => self.cancel(port),
            _ => None,
        };
        Some(reaction.unwrap_or_else(|| self.waiting()))
    }

    /// The HUD rendered proposal `id` (D22). A countdown step starts its
    /// countdown only now; a confirm step may be confirmed from now on.
    pub fn shown(&mut self, port: &dyn InboxPort, id: u64) -> Option<Reaction> {
        match std::mem::take(&mut self.state) {
            State::Presenting { proposal, id: pid } if pid == id => Some(self.enqueue(port, proposal, id)),
            State::Confirm { proposal, id: pid, .. } if pid == id => {
                self.state = State::Confirm { proposal, id, shown: true };
                None
            }
            other => {
                self.state = other;
                None
            }
        }
    }

    /// The HUD did not show proposal `id`: the event failed, or no
    /// acknowledgement came in time. Fail closed (D22).
    pub fn display_failed(&mut self, id: u64) -> Option<Reaction> {
        let waiting = match &self.state {
            State::Presenting { id: pid, .. } => *pid == id,
            State::Confirm { id: pid, shown, .. } => *pid == id && !shown,
            _ => false,
        };
        waiting.then(|| self.error(Refusal::DisplayFailed))
    }

    /// Enter or a click (with the id the HUD shows), or a spoken "confirm"
    /// (`None`). Only a proposal the HUD acknowledged can be confirmed.
    pub fn confirm(&mut self, port: &dyn InboxPort, id: Option<u64>) -> Option<Reaction> {
        match std::mem::take(&mut self.state) {
            State::Confirm { proposal, id: pid, shown: true } if id.is_none_or(|i| i == pid) => {
                Some(self.enqueue(port, proposal, pid))
            }
            other => {
                self.state = other;
                None
            }
        }
    }

    /// A digit or a click on candidate `index` (zero-based).
    pub fn choose(&mut self, index: usize) -> Option<Reaction> {
        let routed = match (&self.state, &self.snapshot) {
            (State::WhichOne(choice), Some(snapshot)) if index < choice.candidates.len() => {
                router::bind(snapshot, choice, index)
            }
            _ => return None,
        };
        self.state = State::Thinking;
        Some(self.take(routed))
    }

    /// Esc or a click on Cancel. A countdown is cancelled by its id (spec
    /// §5.6); a step already sent cannot be undone.
    pub fn cancel(&mut self, port: &dyn InboxPort) -> Option<Reaction> {
        let reaction = match std::mem::take(&mut self.state) {
            State::Presenting { proposal, .. } | State::Confirm { proposal, .. } => {
                self.done(Some(&proposal), OutcomeView::Cancelled)
            }
            State::WhichOne(_) | State::Thinking => self.done(None, OutcomeView::Cancelled),
            State::Counting { proposal, action_id, .. } if port.cancel(&action_id) => {
                self.done(Some(&proposal), OutcomeView::Cancelled)
            }
            State::Counting { proposal, action_id, .. } | State::Sending { proposal, action_id } => {
                let view = self.view(&proposal);
                self.state = State::Sending { proposal, action_id };
                show(Hud::Sending { proposal: view, too_late_to_cancel: true })
            }
            State::Idle | State::Shown => show(Hud::Hidden),
        };
        Some(reaction)
    }

    /// An outcome from acd-client's stream.
    pub fn outcome(&mut self, id: &str, outcome: VoiceOutcome) -> Option<Reaction> {
        if self.current_action() != Some(id) {
            return None;
        }
        let (proposal, action_id) = match std::mem::take(&mut self.state) {
            State::Counting { proposal, action_id, .. } | State::Sending { proposal, action_id } => (proposal, action_id),
            other => {
                self.state = other;
                return None;
            }
        };
        let view = match outcome {
            VoiceOutcome::Fired => {
                let view = self.view(&proposal);
                self.state = State::Sending { proposal, action_id };
                return Some(show(Hud::Sending { proposal: view, too_late_to_cancel: false }));
            }
            VoiceOutcome::Delivered => OutcomeView::Delivered,
            VoiceOutcome::Rejected { code } => OutcomeView::Rejected { code },
            VoiceOutcome::Uncertain => OutcomeView::Uncertain,
            VoiceOutcome::Cancelled => OutcomeView::Cancelled,
            VoiceOutcome::NotSent { reason } => OutcomeView::NotSent { reason },
        };
        Some(self.done(Some(&proposal), view))
    }

    /// The outcome stream lagged. A counting action is cancelled, since its
    /// `Fired` may be lost; if that is too late, or it already fired, its
    /// result is unknown.
    pub fn outcomes_lost(&mut self, port: &dyn InboxPort) -> Option<Reaction> {
        let id = self.current_action()?.to_owned();
        if matches!(self.state, State::Counting { .. }) && port.cancel(&id) {
            return self.outcome(&id, VoiceOutcome::Cancelled);
        }
        self.outcome(&id, VoiceOutcome::Uncertain)
    }

    /// Voice turned off: stop unsent work and drop every voice text held in
    /// memory (spec §6.3). Utterance and proposal ids keep growing, so a
    /// late response or acknowledgement from before matches nothing after.
    pub fn reset(&mut self, port: &dyn InboxPort) {
        if let State::Counting { action_id, .. } = &self.state {
            let _ = port.cancel(action_id);
        }
        self.state = State::Idle;
        self.snapshot = None;
        self.transcript = None;
        self.prior = Prior::Nothing;
        self.utterance += 1;
    }

    fn take(&mut self, routed: Routed) -> Reaction {
        match routed {
            Routed::Refused(refusal) => self.error(refusal),
            Routed::WhichOne(choice) => {
                let hud = self.which_one_hud(&choice);
                self.state = State::WhichOne(choice);
                show(hud)
            }
            Routed::Proposal(p) => match p.level {
                Level::None => self.immediate(p),
                Level::Countdown => {
                    let id = self.next_proposal();
                    let proposal = self.view(&p);
                    self.state = State::Presenting { proposal: p, id };
                    show(Hud::Countdown { proposal, proposal_id: id, action_id: None })
                }
                Level::Confirm => {
                    let id = self.next_proposal();
                    let proposal = self.view(&p);
                    self.state = State::Confirm { proposal: p, id, shown: false };
                    show(Hud::Confirm { proposal, proposal_id: id })
                }
            },
        }
    }

    fn next_proposal(&mut self) -> u64 {
        self.proposals += 1;
        self.proposals
    }

    fn immediate(&mut self, p: Proposal) -> Reaction {
        match &p.step {
            Step::Cancel => {
                return match self.prior {
                    Prior::Cancelled => self.done(None, OutcomeView::Cancelled),
                    Prior::TooLate => self.error(Refusal::AlreadySent),
                    Prior::Nothing => self.error(Refusal::NothingToCancel),
                };
            }
            Step::Select { card } => {
                self.state = State::Shown;
                return Reaction {
                    hud: Hud::Done { proposal: None, outcome: OutcomeView::Selected },
                    select: Some(card.clone()),
                };
            }
            _ => {}
        }
        let text = self.snapshot.as_ref().and_then(|s| match &p.step {
            Step::Read { card } => s.card(card).and_then(speech::read),
            Step::Summarize { machine } => Some(speech::summarize(s, machine.as_deref())),
            _ => None,
        });
        self.state = State::Shown;
        match text {
            Some(text) => show(Hud::Speaking { text }),
            None => self.error(Refusal::NothingToRead),
        }
    }

    fn enqueue(&mut self, port: &dyn InboxPort, p: Proposal, id: u64) -> Reaction {
        let Some(op) = self.snapshot.as_ref().and_then(|s| operation(s, &p)) else {
            return self.error(Refusal::SnapshotChanged);
        };
        match port.enqueue(op) {
            Ok(action_id) => {
                let proposal = self.view(&p);
                self.state = State::Counting { proposal: p, id, action_id: action_id.clone() };
                show(Hud::Countdown { proposal, proposal_id: id, action_id: Some(action_id) })
            }
            Err(e) => self.error(refusal_for(e)),
        }
    }

    /// The HUD for the step that still waits.
    fn waiting(&self) -> Reaction {
        match &self.state {
            State::Confirm { proposal, id, .. } => show(Hud::Confirm { proposal: self.view(proposal), proposal_id: *id }),
            State::WhichOne(choice) => show(self.which_one_hud(choice)),
            _ => show(Hud::Hidden),
        }
    }

    fn which_one_hud(&self, choice: &Choice) -> Hud {
        let candidates = match &self.snapshot {
            Some(s) => choice
                .candidates
                .iter()
                .filter_map(|k| s.card(k))
                .map(|c| target_view(s, c))
                .collect(),
            None => Vec::new(),
        };
        Hud::WhichOne { candidates, transcript: self.transcript.clone().unwrap_or_default() }
    }

    fn done(&mut self, proposal: Option<&Proposal>, outcome: OutcomeView) -> Reaction {
        let proposal = proposal.map(|p| self.view(p));
        self.state = State::Shown;
        show(Hud::Done { proposal, outcome })
    }

    /// "Didn't understand" shows the transcript (spec §9); nothing else does.
    fn error(&mut self, refusal: Refusal) -> Reaction {
        self.state = State::Shown;
        let transcript = if refusal == Refusal::DidNotUnderstand { self.transcript.clone() } else { None };
        show(Hud::Error { refusal, transcript })
    }

    fn view(&self, p: &Proposal) -> ProposalView {
        let snapshot = self.snapshot.as_ref().expect("a proposal comes from a frozen snapshot");
        proposal_view(snapshot, p)
    }
}

/// The operation with its full guard (spec §5.6, B2).
fn operation(snapshot: &Snapshot, p: &Proposal) -> Option<Enqueue> {
    let frozen = snapshot.card(p.step.card()?)?;
    let guard = Guard {
        conn: frozen.conn?,
        agent_id: frozen.agent_id.clone(),
        request: frozen.pending.as_ref().map(|r| (r.id.clone(), r.revision)),
    };
    match &p.step {
        Step::Answer { card, request_id, revision, option_id } => {
            let planned = Some((request_id.clone(), *revision));
            (guard.request == planned).then(|| Enqueue::Pick { card: card.clone(), option_id: option_id.clone(), guard })
        }
        Step::AnswerText { card, request_id, revision, text } => {
            let planned = Some((request_id.clone(), *revision));
            (guard.request == planned).then(|| Enqueue::AnswerText { card: card.clone(), text: text.clone(), guard })
        }
        Step::Send { card, text } => Some(Enqueue::Reply { card: card.clone(), text: text.clone(), guard }),
        Step::Cancel | Step::Read { .. } | Step::Summarize { .. } | Step::Select { .. } => None,
    }
}

fn refusal_for(e: EnqueueError) -> Refusal {
    match e {
        EnqueueError::Stale | EnqueueError::ConnectionChanged | EnqueueError::UnknownCard => Refusal::SnapshotChanged,
        EnqueueError::NotAnswerable => Refusal::ReadOnly,
        EnqueueError::AlreadyCounting => Refusal::AlreadyCounting,
        EnqueueError::BadText => Refusal::BadText,
        EnqueueError::CannotReply => Refusal::CannotReply,
    }
}

fn target_view(snapshot: &Snapshot, card: &CardSnap) -> TargetView {
    TargetView {
        card: card.key.clone(),
        machine: clean(&card.machine_name, LABEL_MAX),
        agent: speech::kind_name(card.kind).to_owned(),
        title: clean(&card.title, TITLE_MAX),
        request: card.pending.as_ref().map(speech::request_label),
        off_screen: snapshot.visibility(card) != Visibility::Visible,
    }
}

fn proposal_view(snapshot: &Snapshot, p: &Proposal) -> ProposalView {
    let card = p
        .step
        .card()
        .and_then(|k| snapshot.card(k))
        .expect("a confirmed or counted step has a card from this snapshot");
    let req = card.pending.as_ref();
    let (action, option, text) = match &p.step {
        Step::Answer { option_id, .. } => {
            (ProposalAction::Answer, req.and_then(|r| r.options.iter().find(|o| &o.id == option_id)), None)
        }
        Step::AnswerText { text, .. } => (ProposalAction::AnswerText, None, Some(text.clone())),
        Step::Send { text, .. } => (ProposalAction::Send, None, Some(text.clone())),
        Step::Cancel | Step::Read { .. } | Step::Summarize { .. } | Step::Select { .. } => {
            unreachable!("safety::level gives these level None, so they are never shown as proposals")
        }
    };
    ProposalView {
        action,
        target: target_view(snapshot, card),
        option: option.map(|o| o.label.clone()),
        effect: option.map(|o| o.effect),
        rule: option
            .filter(|o| o.effect == Effect::AllowAlways)
            .and_then(|o| o.description.clone()),
        question: match action {
            ProposalAction::Send => None,
            ProposalAction::Answer | ProposalAction::AnswerText => {
                req.map(|r| r.question.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| r.detail.clone()))
            }
        },
        text,
        provenance: p.provenance,
        level: p.level,
    }
}
```

### Task 9: `acd-client`: action outcomes, stamped view, full guards and redaction

This task depends on nothing else in the plan and can run in parallel with Tasks 1–8.

**Files:**
- Create: `crates/acd-client/src/voice.rs`
- Modify: `crates/acd-client/Cargo.toml` (dev-dependency `tracing-subscriber`)
- Modify: `crates/acd-client/src/lib.rs` (module list)
- Modify: `crates/acd-client/src/actions.rs`:
  - `remove_invalid` (131–149)
  - `notice` / `check` (158–222)
  - the `TextSemantics` and `ActionKind` derives (39–67)
  - `expire_all`, which is new
- Modify: `crates/acd-client/src/commands.rs`: the `Command` and `Outcome` derives (6–45)
- Modify: `crates/acd-client/src/conn.rs:391`: log the daemon error code, never its message
- Modify: `crates/acd-client/src/inbox.rs`. Line numbers are from `main` at `bf5b748`:
  - imports 1–24
  - `PickError` 36–54
  - `Inbox` 100–150
  - `import_pairing` 234–242
  - `remove_machine` 328–336
  - `set_show_subagents` 372–384
  - `rebuild` 458–504
  - `fire_due` 505–585
  - `on_machine_event` 603–613
  - `end_machine` 668–676
  - `pick` / `answer_text` / `reply` 680–858
  - `cancel` / `escape` 859–872
  - `set_show_idle` 903–909
  - `drop_hidden_actions` 915–935
- Modify: `crates/acd-client/tests/inbox.rs`:
  - the import on line 2
  - `setup` becomes a wrapper around `setup_with(timing)`
  - new tests at the end

**Interfaces:**
- Produces (in `acd_client::voice`):
  - `NotSentReason` with variants `Offline`, `NotSupported`, `AgentGone`, `AnsweredElsewhere`, `Changed`, `AgentBusy` and `Hidden`.
  - `ActionOutcome` with variants `Fired`, `Delivered`, `Rejected { code }`, `Uncertain`, `Cancelled` and `NotSent { reason }`.
  - `ActionEvent { action_id, outcome }`.
  - `ConnStamp { instance, conn_gen }`.
  - `Guard { conn: ConnStamp, agent_id: String, request: Option<(String, u64)> }`.
  - `StampedView { view: InboxView, hidden_idle_cards: Vec<CardView>, stamps: HashMap<String, ConnStamp> }`.
- Produces (on `Inbox`):
  - `action_outcomes()`, `conn_stamp(&str)` and `stamped_view() -> Arc<StampedView>`.
  - `pick_guarded(card_key, option_id, &Guard)`, `answer_text_guarded(card_key, text, &Guard)` and `reply_guarded(card_key, text, &Guard)`, each returning `Result<String, PickError>`.
  - `PickError::ConnectionChanged`.
  - Under `feature = "testing"`: `hold_rebuild_for_test(bool)` and `expire_countdowns_for_test()`.
- Produces (in `acd_client::actions`): `check_reason`, `notice_for` and `check`. `check` keeps its signature and returns the same notices.
- Unchanged: `pick`, `answer_text`, `reply`, `cancel` and `escape` keep their signatures and behaviour. `cancel(action_id)` already exists (D4) and now also emits `Cancelled`. The ts-rs bindings do not change.

Rules:

1. **Outcomes (D1–D3).**
   - Every action id gets at most one terminal outcome.
   - `Fired` is emitted for every action that `Actions::due` removes. `cancel(id)` returns false from then on, so a true cancel and `Fired` exclude each other.
   - Events carry no text.
2. **Stamped view (D5, B1).** `rebuild` publishes the view, the idle cards it hides (D7), and the connection stamps of the very `MachineState`s it built the view from, as one value. A reconnect that `MachineHandle::state` has seen but `rebuild` has not yet published therefore cannot pair new stamps with old cards.
3. **Full guard (B2).** Each guarded operation checks the expected connection, agent and pending request against the live `MachineState`, under the lock that records the action. A send whose agent gained a request is refused. A guarded send also checks `can_send` on the live state, not on the view.
4. **Redaction (B5).**
   - `Debug` on `Command`, `Outcome`, `ActionKind` and `TextSemantics` prints text lengths only.
   - `conn.rs` logs a daemon `error` frame's code, never its message.

```rust
/// Countdowns that never end on their own; tests end them with
/// `expire_countdowns_for_test`, so ordering is decided by the test.
fn held() -> acd_client::Timing {
    acd_client::Timing { undo: Duration::from_secs(3600), ..common::fast() }
}

fn working_agent(id: &str) -> Value {
    let mut a = pending_agent();
    a["id"] = id.into();
    a["state"] = "working".into();
    a["pending"] = Value::Null;
    a
}

async fn next_outcome(rx: &mut tokio::sync::broadcast::Receiver<ActionEvent>, id: &str) -> ActionOutcome {
    tokio::time::timeout(Duration::from_secs(5), async {
        loop {
            let event = rx.recv().await.expect("the outcome stream is open");
            if event.action_id == id {
                return event.outcome;
            }
        }
    })
    .await
    .expect("timed out waiting for an action outcome")
}

async fn answer_frame(f: &FakeServer) -> Value {
    tokio::time::timeout(Duration::from_secs(5), async {
        loop {
            if let Some(v) = f.received().into_iter().find(|v| v["type"] == "request.answer") {
                return v;
            }
            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(10)).await;
        }
    })
    .await
    .expect("no request.answer frame")
}

fn guard_for(ib: &Inbox, card: &str) -> Guard {
    let (machine, agent) = card.split_once('/').unwrap();
    let request = ib
        .view()
        .borrow()
        .cards
        .iter()
        .find(|c| c.key == card)
        .and_then(|c| c.agent.pending.as_ref().map(|r| (r.id.clone(), r.revision)));
    Guard { conn: ib.conn_stamp(machine).unwrap(), agent_id: agent.into(), request }
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_delivered_answer_reports_fired_then_delivered() {
    let (f, c, ib) = setup().await;
    let mut rx = ib.action_outcomes();
    let id = ib.pick("m1/a1", ANSWER_REQ, "allow", 1).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(next_outcome(&mut rx, &id).await, ActionOutcome::Fired);
    let frame = answer_frame(&f).await;
    c.send(serde_json::json!({"type":"result","id":frame["id"],"ok":true,"outcome":"delivered"})).await;
    assert_eq!(next_outcome(&mut rx, &id).await, ActionOutcome::Delivered);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_rejected_answer_reports_its_code() {
    let (f, c, ib) = setup().await;
    let mut rx = ib.action_outcomes();
    let id = ib.pick("m1/a1", ANSWER_REQ, "allow", 1).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(next_outcome(&mut rx, &id).await, ActionOutcome::Fired);
    let frame = answer_frame(&f).await;
    c.send(serde_json::json!({"type":"result","id":frame["id"],"ok":false,"error":{"code":"request_gone","message":"gone"}})).await;
    assert_eq!(next_outcome(&mut rx, &id).await, ActionOutcome::Rejected { code: acd_protocol::ErrorCode::RequestGone });
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn an_answer_lost_across_a_daemon_restart_is_uncertain() {
    let (f, c, ib) = setup().await;
    let mut rx = ib.action_outcomes();
    let id = ib.pick("m1/a1", ANSWER_REQ, "allow", 1).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(next_outcome(&mut rx, &id).await, ActionOutcome::Fired);
    answer_frame(&f).await;
    // A new epoch revokes every in-flight command (commands.rs on_welcome).
    f.set_epoch("e2");
    c.close(1001).await;
    let c2 = f.next_connection().await;
    c2.send(serde_json::json!({"type":"snapshot","epoch":"e2","seq":1,"agents":[]})).await;
    assert_eq!(next_outcome(&mut rx, &id).await, ActionOutcome::Uncertain);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn cancel_and_escape_report_cancelled() {
    let (_f, _c, ib) = setup_with(held()).await;
    let mut rx = ib.action_outcomes();
    let id = ib.pick("m1/a1", ANSWER_REQ, "allow", 1).unwrap();
    assert!(ib.cancel(&id));
    assert_eq!(next_outcome(&mut rx, &id).await, ActionOutcome::Cancelled);
    let id = ib.pick("m1/a1", ANSWER_REQ, "deny", 1).unwrap();
    assert!(ib.escape());
    assert_eq!(next_outcome(&mut rx, &id).await, ActionOutcome::Cancelled);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn dropped_countdowns_report_why_nothing_was_sent() {
    // The request changed.
    let (_f, c, ib) = setup_with(held()).await;
    let mut rx = ib.action_outcomes();
    let id = ib.pick("m1/a1", ANSWER_REQ, "allow", 1).unwrap();
    let mut agent = pending_agent();
    agent["pending"]["revision"] = serde_json::json!(2);
    c.send(serde_json::json!({"type":"agent.updated","epoch":"e1","seq":2,"agent":agent})).await;
    assert_eq!(next_outcome(&mut rx, &id).await, ActionOutcome::NotSent { reason: NotSentReason::Changed });
    // The connection ended.
    let (_f, _c, ib) = setup_with(held()).await;
    let mut rx = ib.action_outcomes();
    let id = ib.pick("m1/a1", ANSWER_REQ, "allow", 1).unwrap();
    ib.drop_connections_for_test();
    assert_eq!(next_outcome(&mut rx, &id).await, ActionOutcome::NotSent { reason: NotSentReason::Offline });
    // The user hid the card.
    let (_f, c, ib) = setup_with(held()).await;
    ib.set_show_subagents(true).unwrap();
    c.send(serde_json::json!({"type":"snapshot","epoch":"e1","seq":2,"agents":[pending_agent(), subagent_agent()]})).await;
    view_until(&ib, "subagent shown", |v| v.cards.len() == 2).await;
    let mut rx = ib.action_outcomes();
    let id = ib.pick("m1/a2", "r2", "allow", 1).unwrap();
    ib.set_show_subagents(false).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(next_outcome(&mut rx, &id).await, ActionOutcome::NotSent { reason: NotSentReason::Hidden });
}

/// Review Focus 5, deterministic half one: a cancelled countdown never
/// fires. A sentinel on the same card proves the timer ran after the cancel.
#[tokio::test]
async fn a_cancelled_countdown_never_fires() {
    let (f, _c, ib) = setup_with(held()).await;
    let mut rx = ib.action_outcomes();
    let id = ib.pick("m1/a1", ANSWER_REQ, "allow", 1).unwrap();
    assert!(ib.cancel(&id));
    ib.expire_countdowns_for_test();
    let sentinel = ib.pick("m1/a1", ANSWER_REQ, "deny", 1).unwrap();
    ib.expire_countdowns_for_test();
    let mut seen = Vec::new();
    tokio::time::timeout(Duration::from_secs(5), async {
        loop {
            let e = rx.recv().await.unwrap();
            let done = e.action_id == sentinel && e.outcome == ActionOutcome::Fired;
            seen.push(e);
            if done {
                break;
            }
        }
    })
    .await
    .expect("the sentinel fired");
    let mine: Vec<ActionOutcome> = seen.iter().filter(|e| e.action_id == id).map(|e| e.outcome).collect();
    assert_eq!(mine, [ActionOutcome::Cancelled]);
    assert_eq!(answer_frame(&f).await["option_id"], "deny", "only the sentinel was sent");
}

/// Review Focus 5, half two: once `Fired` is out, cancel is refused.
#[tokio::test]
async fn a_fired_countdown_cannot_be_cancelled() {
    let (f, _c, ib) = setup_with(held()).await;
    let mut rx = ib.action_outcomes();
    let id = ib.pick("m1/a1", ANSWER_REQ, "allow", 1).unwrap();
    ib.expire_countdowns_for_test();
    assert_eq!(next_outcome(&mut rx, &id).await, ActionOutcome::Fired);
    assert!(!ib.cancel(&id), "the countdown ended");
    assert_eq!(answer_frame(&f).await["option_id"], "allow");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn actions_work_with_no_outcome_subscriber() {
    let (f, _c, ib) = setup_with(held()).await;
    ib.pick("m1/a1", ANSWER_REQ, "allow", 1).unwrap();
    ib.expire_countdowns_for_test();
    assert_eq!(answer_frame(&f).await["option_id"], "allow");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_slow_subscriber_is_told_it_lagged_and_the_stream_goes_on() {
    let (_f, _c, ib) = setup_with(held()).await;
    let mut rx = ib.action_outcomes();
    for _ in 0..300 {
        let id = ib.pick("m1/a1", ANSWER_REQ, "allow", 1).unwrap();
        assert!(ib.cancel(&id));
    }
    assert!(matches!(rx.recv().await, Err(tokio::sync::broadcast::error::RecvError::Lagged(_))));
    let id = ib.pick("m1/a1", ANSWER_REQ, "allow", 1).unwrap();
    assert!(ib.cancel(&id));
    assert_eq!(next_outcome(&mut rx, &id).await, ActionOutcome::Cancelled);
}

/// B1: with `rebuild` held across a reconnect, the stamped view keeps the
/// stamps of the cards it shows; a step planned on it is then refused by
/// the live connection. Released, both move together.
#[tokio::test]
async fn the_stamped_view_never_pairs_new_stamps_with_old_cards() {
    let (f, c, ib) = setup().await;
    let before = ib.stamped_view();
    assert_eq!(before.stamps.get("m1").copied(), ib.conn_stamp("m1"));
    ib.hold_rebuild_for_test(true);
    c.close(4408).await;
    let c2 = f.next_connection().await;
    c2.send(serde_json::json!({"type":"snapshot","epoch":"e1","seq":1,"agents":[pending_agent()]})).await;
    tokio::time::timeout(Duration::from_secs(5), async {
        while ib.conn_stamp("m1") == before.stamps.get("m1").copied() {
            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(10)).await;
        }
    })
    .await
    .expect("the live connection moved on");
    let held = ib.stamped_view();
    assert_eq!(held, before, "stamps stay with the cards they were published with");
    let planned = Guard {
        conn: held.stamps["m1"],
        agent_id: "a1".into(),
        request: Some((ANSWER_REQ.into(), 1)),
    };
    assert_eq!(ib.pick_guarded("m1/a1", "allow", &planned), Err(PickError::ConnectionChanged));
    ib.hold_rebuild_for_test(false);
    tokio::time::timeout(Duration::from_secs(5), async {
        loop {
            let now = ib.stamped_view();
            if now.stamps.get("m1").copied() == ib.conn_stamp("m1") && now.view.cards.iter().any(|c| c.answerable) {
                break;
            }
            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(10)).await;
        }
    })
    .await
    .expect("released: view and stamps move together");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn the_stamped_view_carries_the_idle_cards_the_view_hides() {
    let (_f, c, ib) = setup().await;
    let mut idle = working_agent("a3");
    idle["state"] = "idle".into();
    c.send(serde_json::json!({"type":"agent.updated","epoch":"e1","seq":2,"agent":idle})).await;
    tokio::time::timeout(Duration::from_secs(5), async {
        while !ib.stamped_view().hidden_idle_cards.iter().any(|c| c.key == "m1/a3") {
            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(10)).await;
        }
    })
    .await
    .expect("the hidden idle card");
    assert!(!ib.view().borrow().cards.iter().any(|c| c.key == "m1/a3"), "the panel still hides it");
}

/// B2: the full guard. Connection, request appearing, disappearing and
/// changing revision are each refused; the current guard passes.
#[tokio::test]
async fn guarded_operations_check_connection_agent_and_request() {
    let (f, c, ib) = setup_with(held()).await;
    // The connection changed.
    let old = guard_for(&ib, "m1/a1");
    c.close(4408).await;
    let c = f.next_connection().await;
    c.send(serde_json::json!({"type":"snapshot","epoch":"e1","seq":1,"agents":[pending_agent(), working_agent("a2")]})).await;
    tokio::time::timeout(Duration::from_secs(5), async {
        while ib.conn_stamp("m1") == Some(old.conn) || ib.view().borrow().cards.len() < 2 {
            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(10)).await;
        }
    })
    .await
    .expect("reconnected");
    assert_eq!(ib.pick_guarded("m1/a1", "allow", &old), Err(PickError::ConnectionChanged));
    // A request appeared on an agent planned without one.
    let quiet = guard_for(&ib, "m1/a2");
    assert_eq!(quiet.request, None);
    let mut busy = pending_agent();
    busy["id"] = "a2".into();
    busy["pending"]["id"] = "r9".into();
    busy["pending"]["agent_id"] = "a2".into();
    c.send(serde_json::json!({"type":"agent.updated","epoch":"e1","seq":2,"agent":busy})).await;
    view_until(&ib, "a2 needs you", |v| v.cards.iter().any(|c| c.key == "m1/a2" && c.agent.pending.is_some())).await;
    assert_eq!(ib.reply_guarded("m1/a2", "go on".into(), &quiet), Err(PickError::StaleRequest));
    // The revision changed.
    let planned = guard_for(&ib, "m1/a1");
    let mut bumped = pending_agent();
    bumped["pending"]["revision"] = serde_json::json!(2);
    c.send(serde_json::json!({"type":"agent.updated","epoch":"e1","seq":3,"agent":bumped})).await;
    view_until(&ib, "revision 2", |v| v.cards.iter().any(|c| c.agent.pending.as_ref().is_some_and(|p| p.revision == 2))).await;
    assert_eq!(ib.pick_guarded("m1/a1", "allow", &planned), Err(PickError::StaleRequest));
    assert_eq!(ib.answer_text_guarded("m1/a1", "x".into(), &planned), Err(PickError::StaleRequest));
    // The request disappeared.
    let planned = guard_for(&ib, "m1/a1");
    let mut gone = pending_agent();
    gone["pending"] = Value::Null;
    c.send(serde_json::json!({"type":"agent.updated","epoch":"e1","seq":4,"agent":gone})).await;
    view_until(&ib, "answered", |v| v.cards.iter().any(|c| c.key == "m1/a1" && c.agent.pending.is_none())).await;
    assert_eq!(ib.pick_guarded("m1/a1", "allow", &planned), Err(PickError::StaleRequest));
    assert!(ib.view().borrow().actions.is_empty());
    // The current guard passes.
    let now = guard_for(&ib, "m1/a2");
    ib.pick_guarded("m1/a2", "allow", &now).unwrap();
}

/// D7 at the client: a guarded send reaches an idle agent the panel hides
/// (no published card); the keyboard path still needs a card on screen.
#[tokio::test]
async fn a_guarded_send_reaches_a_hidden_idle_agent() {
    let (f, c, ib) = setup().await;
    let mut idle = working_agent("a3");
    idle["state"] = "idle".into();
    c.send(serde_json::json!({"type":"agent.updated","epoch":"e1","seq":2,"agent":idle})).await;
    tokio::time::timeout(Duration::from_secs(5), async {
        while !ib.stamped_view().hidden_idle_cards.iter().any(|c| c.key == "m1/a3") {
            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(10)).await;
        }
    })
    .await
    .expect("the hidden idle card");
    assert_eq!(ib.reply("m1/a3", "x".into()), Err(PickError::UnknownCard), "keyboard path unchanged");
    let guard = Guard { conn: ib.conn_stamp("m1").unwrap(), agent_id: "a3".into(), request: None };
    ib.reply_guarded("m1/a3", "rebase".into(), &guard).unwrap();
    let sent = tokio::time::timeout(Duration::from_secs(5), async {
        loop {
            if let Some(v) = f.received().into_iter().find(|v| v["type"] == "agent.send") {
                return v;
            }
            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(10)).await;
        }
    })
    .await
    .expect("sent");
    assert_eq!((sent["agent_id"].as_str(), sent["text"].as_str()), (Some("a3"), Some("rebase")));
}

#[test]
fn debug_output_shows_text_lengths_only() {
    use acd_client::actions::{ActionKind, TextSemantics};
    use acd_client::commands::{Command, Outcome};
    let shown = [
        format!("{:?}", Command::Send { agent_id: "a".into(), text: "SECRET-1".into() }),
        format!("{:?}", Command::AnswerText { request_id: "r".into(), revision: 1, text: "SECRET-2".into() }),
        format!("{:?}", Outcome::Failed { code: acd_protocol::ErrorCode::BadRequest, message: "SECRET-3".into() }),
        format!("{:?}", ActionKind::Reply { text: "SECRET-4".into() }),
    ];
    for s in shown {
        assert!(!s.contains("SECRET"), "{s}");
    }
    let req: acd_protocol::Request = serde_json::from_value(pending_agent()["pending"].clone()).unwrap();
    assert!(!format!("{:?}", TextSemantics::of(&req, "SECRET-5")).contains("SECRET"));
}

/// B5 through the real transport: a daemon error frame's message never
/// reaches the logs. Current-thread runtime: `set_default` is per thread.
#[tokio::test(flavor = "current_thread")]
async fn daemon_error_messages_never_reach_the_logs() {
    use std::sync::Mutex as StdMutex;
    #[derive(Clone, Default)]
    struct Buffer(Arc<StdMutex<Vec<u8>>>);
    impl std::io::Write for Buffer {
        fn write(&mut self, bytes: &[u8]) -> std::io::Result<usize> {
            self.0.lock().unwrap().extend_from_slice(bytes);
            Ok(bytes.len())
        }
        fn flush(&mut self) -> std::io::Result<()> {
            Ok(())
        }
    }
    let buffer = Buffer::default();
    let writer = buffer.clone();
    let subscriber = tracing_subscriber::fmt()
        .with_max_level(tracing::Level::TRACE)
        .with_ansi(false)
        .with_writer(move || writer.clone())
        .finish();
    let _guard = tracing::subscriber::set_default(subscriber);
    let (f, c, ib) = setup().await;
    c.send(serde_json::json!({"type":"error","code":"bad_request","message":"SECRET-DAEMON echo of run the tests"})).await;
    let mut rx = ib.action_outcomes();
    let id = ib.reply("m1/a1", "SECRET-REPLY".into()).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(next_outcome(&mut rx, &id).await, ActionOutcome::Fired);
    let sent = tokio::time::timeout(Duration::from_secs(5), async {
        loop {
            if let Some(v) = f.received().into_iter().find(|v| v["type"] == "agent.send") {
                return v;
            }
            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(10)).await;
        }
    })
    .await
    .unwrap();
    c.send(serde_json::json!({"type":"result","id":sent["id"],"ok":false,"error":{"code":"agent_busy","message":"SECRET-RESULT"}})).await;
    next_outcome(&mut rx, &id).await;
    let logs = String::from_utf8(buffer.0.lock().unwrap().clone()).unwrap();
    assert!(logs.contains("daemon error"), "the capture works: {logs}");
    assert!(!logs.contains("SECRET"), "{logs}");
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Add the voice support types**

Create `crates/acd-client/src/voice.rs`:

```rust
//! What a voice client needs from the inbox (voice spec §4.3, §5.6):
//! per-action outcomes, connection stamps, operation guards, and a view
//! published together with the stamps it was built from.
//!
//! Every action id gets at most one terminal outcome. `Fired` comes first
//! when the countdown ended; from then on `Inbox::cancel` returns false.
use std::collections::HashMap;

use acd_protocol::ErrorCode;

use crate::commands::{NotSent, Outcome};
use crate::view::{CardView, InboxView};

/// Why an action ended without being sent.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum NotSentReason {
    Offline,
    NotSupported,
    AgentGone,
    AnsweredElsewhere,
    Changed,
    AgentBusy,
    /// The user hid the card (subagents or idle agents turned off).
    Hidden,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum ActionOutcome {
    Fired,
    Delivered,
    Rejected { code: ErrorCode },
    Uncertain,
    Cancelled,
    NotSent { reason: NotSentReason },
}

impl ActionOutcome {
    pub fn is_terminal(self) -> bool {
        self != ActionOutcome::Fired
    }

    pub(crate) fn from_submit(outcome: &Outcome) -> Self {
        match outcome {
            Outcome::Delivered | Outcome::Done => Self::Delivered,
            Outcome::Failed { code, .. } => Self::Rejected { code: *code },
            Outcome::Uncertain => Self::Uncertain,
            Outcome::NotSent(NotSent::Offline) => Self::NotSent { reason: NotSentReason::Offline },
            Outcome::NotSent(NotSent::NotAdvertised) => Self::NotSent { reason: NotSentReason::NotSupported },
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct ActionEvent {
    pub action_id: String,
    pub outcome: ActionOutcome,
}

/// A machine connection's identity: handle instance and connection
/// generation. Kept out of the view, which is a ts-rs binding.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct ConnStamp {
    pub instance: u64,
    pub conn_gen: u64,
}

/// Everything an operation was planned on: the connection, the agent, and
/// its pending request (`None`: there was none).
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Guard {
    pub conn: ConnStamp,
    pub agent_id: String,
    pub request: Option<(String, u64)>,
}

/// One rebuild's output: the view, the idle cards it hides, and the
/// connection stamps of the states it was built from.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct StampedView {
    pub view: InboxView,
    pub hidden_idle_cards: Vec<CardView>,
    pub stamps: HashMap<String, ConnStamp>,
}
```

```rust
/// Typed text is the user's (voice spec §3.7): `Debug` shows its length.
impl fmt::Debug for TextSemantics {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        f.debug_struct("TextSemantics")
            .field("request_id", &self.request_id)
            .field("revision", &self.revision)
            .field("kind", &self.kind)
            .field("text_bytes", &self.text.len())
            .finish_non_exhaustive()
    }
}

impl fmt::Debug for ActionKind {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        match self {
            ActionKind::Answer(s) => f.debug_tuple("Answer").field(s).finish(),
            ActionKind::AnswerText(s) => f.debug_tuple("AnswerText").field(s).finish(),
            ActionKind::Reply { text } => f.debug_struct("Reply").field("text_bytes", &text.len()).finish(),
        }
    }
```

3. Replace `remove_invalid` (131–149):

```rust
    pub(crate) fn remove_invalid(
        &mut self,
        machine_key: &str,
        mut invalid: impl FnMut(&Action) -> Option<NotSentReason>,
    ) -> Vec<(Action, NotSentReason)> {
        let mut removed = Vec::new();
        let mut i = 0;
        while i < self.items.len() {
            if self.items[i].machine_key == machine_key
                && let Some(reason) = invalid(&self.items[i])
            {
                removed.push((self.items.remove(i), reason));
                continue;
            }
            i += 1;
        }
        removed
    }

    /// Test hook: every countdown ends now.
    #[cfg(feature = "testing")]
    pub(crate) fn expire_all(&mut self, now: Instant) {
        for a in &mut self.items {
            a.fires_at = now;
        }
    }
```

4. Replace `check` (164–222) with `notice_for`, a delegating `check`, and `check_reason`. Keep the `notice` helper at 158–163:

```rust
/// The card notice for an action that ended without being sent.
pub fn notice_for(reason: NotSentReason) -> Notice {
    match reason {
        NotSentReason::Offline => notice("Offline. Nothing was sent.", NoticeTone::Warning),
        NotSentReason::NotSupported => notice("Not supported for this agent", NoticeTone::Info),
        NotSentReason::AgentGone => notice("The agent is gone", NoticeTone::Info),
        NotSentReason::AnsweredElsewhere => notice("Answered elsewhere", NoticeTone::Info),
        NotSentReason::Changed => notice("The request changed. Nothing was sent.", NoticeTone::Warning),
        NotSentReason::AgentBusy => notice("The agent is busy. Try again when it is idle.", NoticeTone::Warning),
        NotSentReason::Hidden => notice("Hidden. Nothing was sent.", NoticeTone::Info),
    }
}

pub fn check(a: &Action, state: &MachineState) -> Result<(), Notice> {
    check_reason(a, state).map_err(notice_for)
}

pub fn check_reason(a: &Action, state: &MachineState) -> Result<(), NotSentReason> {
    if state.instance != a.instance
        || state.conn_gen != a.conn_gen
        || state.status != MachineStatus::Online
    {
        return Err(NotSentReason::Offline);
    }
    let cap = match a.kind {
        ActionKind::Answer(_) | ActionKind::AnswerText(_) => "answer",
        ActionKind::Reply { .. } => "send",
    };
    if !state.session.has(cap) {
        return Err(NotSentReason::NotSupported);
    }
    let Some(agent) = state.session.agents.get(&a.agent_id) else {
        return Err(NotSentReason::AgentGone);
    };
    match &a.kind {
        ActionKind::Answer(sem) => {
            let Some(req) = agent.pending.as_ref() else {
                return Err(NotSentReason::AnsweredElsewhere);
            };
            if req.id != sem.request_id || req.status != RequestStatus::Open {
                return Err(NotSentReason::AnsweredElsewhere);
            }
            if !req.is_answerable()
                || Semantics::of(req, &sem.option.id).as_ref() != Some(sem)
                || sem.option.effect == Effect::Unknown
            {
                return Err(NotSentReason::Changed);
            }
        }
        ActionKind::AnswerText(sem) => {
            let Some(req) = agent.pending.as_ref() else {
                return Err(NotSentReason::AnsweredElsewhere);
            };
            if req.id != sem.request_id || req.status != RequestStatus::Open {
                return Err(NotSentReason::AnsweredElsewhere);
            }
            if !req.is_answerable() || !req.free_text || TextSemantics::of(req, &sem.text) != *sem {
                return Err(NotSentReason::Changed);
            }
        }
        ActionKind::Reply { .. } if !agent.can_send => {
            return Err(NotSentReason::AgentBusy);
        }
        _ => {}
    }
    Ok(())
}
```

```rust
/// Typed text and daemon messages stay out of `Debug` (voice spec §3.7):
/// lengths only.
impl fmt::Debug for Command {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        match self {
            Self::Answer { request_id, revision, option_id } => f
                .debug_struct("Answer")
                .field("request_id", request_id)
                .field("revision", revision)
                .field("option_id", option_id)
                .finish(),
            Self::AnswerText { request_id, revision, text } => f
                .debug_struct("AnswerText")
                .field("request_id", request_id)
                .field("revision", revision)
                .field("text_bytes", &text.len())
                .finish(),
            Self::Send { agent_id, text } => f
                .debug_struct("Send")
                .field("agent_id", agent_id)
                .field("text_bytes", &text.len())
                .finish(),
            Self::MarkSeen { agent_id } => f.debug_struct("MarkSeen").field("agent_id", agent_id).finish(),
        }
    }
}

impl fmt::Debug for Outcome {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        match self {
            Self::Delivered => f.write_str("Delivered"),
            Self::Done => f.write_str("Done"),
            Self::Failed { code, message } => f
                .debug_struct("Failed")
                .field("code", code)
                .field("message_bytes", &message.len())
                .finish(),
            Self::Uncertain => f.write_str("Uncertain"),
            Self::NotSent(n) => f.debug_tuple("NotSent").field(n).finish(),
        }
    }
}
```

5. Add methods after `events()`:

```rust
    /// Outcomes of every countdown action (voice spec §5.6). Subscribe
    /// before starting an action. A lagged receiver must cancel or treat as
    /// uncertain the actions it watches.
    pub fn action_outcomes(&self) -> broadcast::Receiver<ActionEvent> {
        self.outcomes_tx.subscribe()
    }

    fn emit(&self, action_id: &str, outcome: ActionOutcome) {
        let _ = self.outcomes_tx.send(ActionEvent { action_id: action_id.to_owned(), outcome });
    }

    /// The view, the idle cards it hides, and the stamps of the machine
    /// states it was built from, all from one rebuild (voice plan D5).
    pub fn stamped_view(&self) -> Arc<StampedView> {
        self.stamped_tx.borrow().clone()
    }

    /// The machine's live connection.
    pub fn conn_stamp(&self, machine_key: &str) -> Option<ConnStamp> {
        let handle = {
            let inner = self.inner.lock().unwrap();
            inner.machines.iter().find(|m| m.entry.key == machine_key)?.handle.clone()?
        };
        let state = handle.state();
        let s = state.borrow();
        Some(ConnStamp { instance: s.instance, conn_gen: s.conn_gen })
    }

    /// Test hook: hold `rebuild`, as if the timer loop were slow.
    #[cfg(feature = "testing")]
    pub fn hold_rebuild_for_test(&self, held: bool) {
        self.rebuild_held.store(held, Ordering::SeqCst);
        self.wake();
    }

    /// Test hook: end every countdown now.
    #[cfg(feature = "testing")]
    pub fn expire_countdowns_for_test(&self) {
        self.inner.lock().unwrap().actions.expire_all(Instant::now());
        self.wake();
    }
```

```rust
fn drop_hidden_actions(inner: &mut Inner) -> Vec<Action> {
    let (show_subagents, show_idle) = (inner.settings.show_subagents, inner.settings.show_idle);
    let states: Vec<(String, MachineState)> = inner
        .machines
        .iter()
        .filter_map(|m| {
            m.handle
                .as_ref()
                .map(|h| (m.entry.key.clone(), h.state().borrow().clone()))
        })
        .collect();
    let mut dropped = Vec::new();
    for (key, state) in &states {
        dropped.extend(inner.actions.remove_where(|a| {
            a.machine_key == *key
                && state.session.agents.get(&a.agent_id).is_some_and(|agent| {
                    (!show_subagents && agent.role == acd_protocol::AgentRole::Subagent)
                        || (!show_idle && view::group(agent) == view::CardGroup::Idle)
                })
        }));
    }
    dropped
}
```

9. Replace `fire_due` (505–585), and add `not_sent` next to `set_notice`:

```rust
    async fn fire_due(self: &Arc<Self>) {
        let due = { self.inner.lock().unwrap().actions.due(Instant::now()) };
        for action in due {
            // The countdown ended: `cancel(id)` can no longer stop it.
            self.emit(&action.id, ActionOutcome::Fired);
            let data = {
                let inner = self.inner.lock().unwrap();
                let m = inner
                    .machines
                    .iter()
                    .find(|m| m.entry.key == action.machine_key);
                m.and_then(|m| {
                    m.handle.clone().map(|h| {
                        let state = h.state().borrow().clone();
                        (h, state)
                    })
                })
            };
            let Some((handle, state)) = data else {
                self.not_sent(&action, NotSentReason::Offline).await;
                continue;
            };
            if let Err(reason) = check_reason(&action, &state) {
                self.not_sent(&action, reason).await;
                continue;
            }
            let validation_action = action.clone();
            let cmd = match action.kind {
                ActionKind::Answer(s) => Command::Answer {
                    request_id: s.request_id,
                    revision: s.revision,
                    option_id: s.option.id,
                },
                ActionKind::AnswerText(s) => Command::AnswerText {
                    request_id: s.request_id,
                    revision: s.revision,
                    text: s.text,
                },
                ActionKind::Reply { text } => Command::Send {
                    agent_id: action.agent_id.clone(),
                    text,
                },
            };
            {
                self.inner
                    .lock()
                    .unwrap()
                    .busy
                    .insert(action.card_key.clone());
            }
            let ib = self.clone();
            tokio::spawn(async move {
                let latest = { handle.state().borrow().clone() };
                let out = match check_reason(&validation_action, &latest) {
                    Ok(()) => Some(handle.submit(cmd, Some(action.conn_gen)).await),
                    Err(reason) => {
                        ib.emit(&validation_action.id, ActionOutcome::NotSent { reason });
                        let mut inner = ib.inner.lock().unwrap();
                        inner.notices.insert(
                            validation_action.card_key.clone(),
                            (notice_for(reason), Instant::now() + ib.opts.timing.notice_ttl),
                        );
                        None
                    }
                };
                if let Some(out) = &out {
                    ib.emit(&validation_action.id, ActionOutcome::from_submit(out));
                }
                let mut inner = ib.inner.lock().unwrap();
                inner.busy.remove(&action.card_key);
                if let Some(out) = out
                    && let Some(n) = view::notice_for_outcome(&out)
                {
                    inner.notices.insert(
                        action.card_key,
                        (n, Instant::now() + ib.opts.timing.notice_ttl),
                    );
                }
                drop(inner);
                ib.wake();
            });
        }
    }

    /// An action that ended without being sent: notice and outcome (D1).
    async fn not_sent(&self, action: &Action, reason: NotSentReason) {
        self.emit(&action.id, ActionOutcome::NotSent { reason });
        self.set_notice(&action.card_key, notice_for(reason)).await;
    }
```

10. Replace `cancel` and `escape` (859–872):

```rust
    pub fn cancel(&self, id: &str) -> bool {
        let removed = self.inner.lock().unwrap().actions.cancel(id);
        let Some(action) = removed else {
            return false;
        };
        self.emit(&action.id, ActionOutcome::Cancelled);
        self.wake();
        true
    }
    pub fn escape(&self) -> bool {
        let removed = self.inner.lock().unwrap().actions.cancel_latest();
        let Some(action) = removed else {
            return false;
        };
        self.emit(&action.id, ActionOutcome::Cancelled);
        self.wake();
        true
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Guarded operations**

Add this free function next to `find_card`:

```rust
/// Refuses an operation whose plan no longer matches the machine (voice
/// spec §5.6): connection, agent, and pending request or its absence.
/// Checked on the live machine state, under the lock that records the
/// action, never on the published view.
fn guard(state: &MachineState, card_key: &str, expect: Option<&Guard>) -> Result<(), PickError> {
    let Some(g) = expect else {
        return Ok(());
    };
    if (state.instance, state.conn_gen) != (g.conn.instance, g.conn.conn_gen) {
        return Err(PickError::ConnectionChanged);
    }
    if card_key.split_once('/').map(|(_, a)| a) != Some(g.agent_id.as_str()) {
        return Err(PickError::UnknownCard);
    }
    let agent = state.session.agents.get(&g.agent_id).ok_or(PickError::StaleRequest)?;
    let live = agent.pending.as_ref().map(|r| (r.id.clone(), r.revision));
    if live != g.request {
        return Err(PickError::StaleRequest);
    }
    Ok(())
}
```

Rename the three bodies. The public signatures stay:

- `pub fn pick(...)` becomes `fn pick_with(self: &Arc<Self>, card_key: &str, request_id: &str, option_id: &str, revision: u64, expect: Option<&Guard>) -> Result<String, PickError>`. Insert `guard(&state, card_key, expect)?;` right after `let state = handle.state().borrow().clone();`.
- `pub fn answer_text(...)` becomes `fn answer_text_with(self: &Arc<Self>, card_key: &str, request_id: &str, revision: u64, text: String, expect: Option<&Guard>)`. Insert `guard(&state, card_key, expect)?;` after its `let state = ...clone();`.
- `pub fn reply(...)` (819–858) is replaced by `reply_with`, written out in full below. The unguarded branch is the existing body unchanged: the published card and `card.can_reply`. The guarded branch never reads the published view. It takes the agent from the guard and checks it on the live machine state, including live `can_send`. That way a send reaches an idle agent the panel hides (D7) and never trusts a stale view (B2):

```rust
    fn reply_with(self: &Arc<Self>, card_key: &str, text: String, expect: Option<&Guard>) -> Result<String, PickError> {
        if text.trim().is_empty() || text.len() > 64 * 1024 {
            return Err(PickError::BadText);
        }
        let mut inner = self.inner.lock().unwrap();
        let (machine_key, handle, _) = find_card(&inner, card_key)?;
        let state = handle.state().borrow().clone();
        guard(&state, card_key, expect)?;
        let agent_id = match expect {
            // Voice: live state only; no published card is needed (D7, B2).
            Some(g) => {
                let live = state.status == conn::MachineStatus::Online
                    && state.session.has("send")
                    && state.session.agents.get(&g.agent_id).is_some_and(|a| a.can_send);
                if !live {
                    return Err(PickError::CannotReply);
                }
                g.agent_id.clone()
            }
            // Keyboard: unchanged.
            None => {
                let current_view = self.view_tx.borrow().clone();
                let card = current_view
                    .cards
                    .iter()
                    .find(|c| c.key == card_key)
                    .ok_or(PickError::UnknownCard)?;
                if !card.can_reply {
                    return Err(PickError::CannotReply);
                }
                card.agent.id.clone()
            }
        };
        let id = uuid::Uuid::new_v4().to_string();
        let now = Instant::now();
        let detail: String = text.chars().take(80).collect();
        inner.actions.add(Action {
            id: id.clone(),
            card_key: card_key.into(),
            machine_key,
            agent_id,
            instance: state.instance,
            conn_gen: state.conn_gen,
            kind: ActionKind::Reply { text },
            fires_at: now + self.opts.timing.undo,
            view: ActionView {
                id: id.clone(),
                card_key: card_key.into(),
                label: "Reply".into(),
                detail: Some(detail),
                fires_at_ms: now_ms() + self.opts.timing.undo.as_secs_f64() * 1000.,
            },
        })?;
        drop(inner);
        self.wake();
        Ok(id)
    }
```

`find_card` (942–954) already resolves the card key from the machine's live session, not from the view, so it accepts a hidden idle agent.

Add the public wrappers, moving the existing doc comment of `answer_text` onto the new public `answer_text`:

```rust
    pub fn pick(self: &Arc<Self>, card_key: &str, request_id: &str, option_id: &str, revision: u64) -> Result<String, PickError> {
        self.pick_with(card_key, request_id, option_id, revision, None)
    }
    /// `pick` on the request `guard` names, refused unless connection,
    /// agent and request still match (voice spec §5.6).
    pub fn pick_guarded(self: &Arc<Self>, card_key: &str, option_id: &str, guard: &Guard) -> Result<String, PickError> {
        let Some((request_id, revision)) = guard.request.clone() else {
            return Err(PickError::StaleRequest);
        };
        self.pick_with(card_key, &request_id, option_id, revision, Some(guard))
    }
    /// A typed answer to a free-text request, with the same undo countdown as
    /// `pick`. The text is trimmed; blank, oversize (16 KiB) or control-char
    /// text is `BadAnswerText`.
    pub fn answer_text(self: &Arc<Self>, card_key: &str, request_id: &str, revision: u64, text: String) -> Result<String, PickError> {
        self.answer_text_with(card_key, request_id, revision, text, None)
    }
    pub fn answer_text_guarded(self: &Arc<Self>, card_key: &str, text: String, guard: &Guard) -> Result<String, PickError> {
        let Some((request_id, revision)) = guard.request.clone() else {
            return Err(PickError::StaleRequest);
        };
        self.answer_text_with(card_key, &request_id, revision, text, Some(guard))
    }
    pub fn reply(self: &Arc<Self>, card_key: &str, text: String) -> Result<String, PickError> {
        self.reply_with(card_key, text, None)
    }
    /// `reply`, refused unless connection, agent and request (including
    /// "no request") still match what the step was planned on.
    pub fn reply_guarded(self: &Arc<Self>, card_key: &str, text: String, guard: &Guard) -> Result<String, PickError> {
        self.reply_with(card_key, text, Some(guard))
    }
```

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use acd_client::view::{TrayColor, TraySummary};
    use acd_protocol::{AgentKind, Effect};
    use serde_json::json;

    fn question_card() -> CardView {
        let agent = serde_json::from_value(json!({
            "id": "a1", "machine_id": "m", "source": "t3", "kind": "codex", "title": "Pick",
            "cwd": null, "project": "app", "state": "needs_you",
            "state_since": "2026-10-08T00:00:00.000Z", "failed": false, "last_message": null,
            "can_send": true, "role": "main",
            "pending": {
                "id": "r1", "revision": 3, "agent_id": "a1", "kind": "question", "status": "open",
                "answer_path": "t3", "title": "Colour", "detail": "Which colour?",
                "options": [
                    {"id": "red", "label": "Red", "description": null, "effect": "choose"},
                    {"id": "odd", "label": "?", "description": null, "effect": "weird"}
                ],
                "questions": [{"id": "q", "header": null, "text": "Which colour, really?", "multi_select": false, "options": []}],
                "free_text": true, "raw_screen": null, "opened_at": "2026-10-08T00:00:00.000Z"
            }
        }))
        .unwrap();
        CardView {
            key: "m1/a1".into(),
            machine_key: "m1".into(),
            machine_name: "Box".into(),
            agent,
            group: CardGroup::Request,
            live: true,
            offline_since_ms: None,
            answerable: true,
            free_text_answerable: true,
            selectable_options: vec!["red".into()],
            can_reply: false,
            can_mark_seen: false,
            busy: false,
            notice: None,
            action: None,
        }
    }

    fn stamped(cards: Vec<CardView>, hidden_idle_cards: Vec<CardView>, stamps: HashMap<String, ClientStamp>) -> StampedView {
        StampedView {
            view: InboxView {
                machines: vec![],
                cards,
                hidden_idle: hidden_idle_cards.len() as u32,
                show_idle: false,
                show_subagents: true,
                tray: TraySummary { color: TrayColor::Grey, needs_you: 0 },
                actions: vec![],
            },
            hidden_idle_cards,
            stamps,
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn freezing_copies_what_voice_needs() {
        let stamps = HashMap::from([("m1".to_owned(), ClientStamp { instance: 4, conn_gen: 7 })]);
        let s = freeze(&stamped(vec![question_card()], vec![], stamps), Presentation::default(), true);
        assert!(s.panel_open && s.show_subagents);
        let c = &s.cards[0];
        assert_eq!((c.kind, c.project.as_deref(), c.group, c.online, c.idle_hidden), (AgentKind::Codex, Some("app"), Group::Request, true, false));
        assert_eq!(c.conn, Some(ConnStamp { instance: 4, conn_gen: 7 }));
        let r = c.pending.as_ref().unwrap();
        assert_eq!((r.revision, r.question.as_deref()), (3, Some("Which colour, really?")));
        assert!(r.answerable && r.free_text);
        assert!(r.options[0].selectable);
        assert_eq!((r.options[1].effect, r.options[1].selectable), (Effect::Unknown, false));
    }

    #[test]
    fn hidden_idle_cards_are_frozen_as_such_and_unstamped_machines_have_no_stamp() {
        let mut idle = question_card();
        idle.key = "m1/a9".into();
        idle.group = CardGroup::Idle;
        let s = freeze(&stamped(vec![question_card()], vec![idle], HashMap::new()), Presentation::default(), false);
        assert_eq!(s.cards.len(), 2);
        assert!(s.cards[1].idle_hidden);
        assert_eq!(s.cards[0].conn, None);
    }
}
```
