```rust
#![allow(dead_code)]
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::Duration;

use acd_client::Timing;
use acd_client::fake::{FakeConn, FakeServer, Identity};
use acd_client::inbox::{Inbox, InboxOptions};
use acd_client::settings::{
    DEFAULT_HOTKEY, EndpointConfig, MachineEntry, MachineSecret, MemorySecrets, SecretStore, Settings,
};
use acd_client::view::InboxView;
use acd_voice::snapshot::Presentation;
use serde_json::{Value, json};

pub const TOKEN: &str = "AAECAwQFBgcICQoLDA0ODxAREhMUFRYXGBkaGxwdHh8";

pub fn fast() -> Timing {
    let ms = Duration::from_millis;
    Timing {
        backoff_base: ms(20),
        backoff_max: ms(100),
        identity_retry: ms(200),
        idle_ping: ms(500),
        ping_timeout: ms(300),
        undo: ms(200),
        not_ready_interval: ms(50),
        connect_total: ms(3000),
        ..Timing::default()
    }
}

/// Machine "Box" (key m1), Claude agent a1 with request r1: Yes / No / Yes, always.
pub fn pending_agent() -> Value {
    json!({"id":"a1","machine_id":"m","source":"herdr","kind":"claude","title":"agent","cwd":null,"project":null,"state":"needs_you","state_since":"2026-10-08T00:00:00.000Z","failed":false,"last_message":null,"can_send":true,"pending":{"id":"r1","revision":1,"agent_id":"a1","kind":"permission","status":"open","answer_path":"hook","title":"Bash","detail":"npm test","options":[{"id":"allow","label":"Yes","description":null,"effect":"allow"},{"id":"deny","label":"No","description":null,"effect":"deny"},{"id":"always","label":"Yes, always","description":"Bash(npm test:*)","effect":"allow_always"}],"questions":null,"free_text":false,"raw_screen":null,"opened_at":"2026-10-08T00:00:00.000Z"}})
}

/// A working Claude agent a2 that takes a reply.
pub fn working_agent() -> Value {
    let mut a = pending_agent();
    a["id"] = "a2".into();
    a["state"] = "working".into();
    a["pending"] = Value::Null;
    a
}

/// An idle Codex agent a3; the panel hides it ("Show idle" is off).
pub fn idle_agent() -> Value {
    let mut a = working_agent();
    a["id"] = "a3".into();
    a["kind"] = "codex".into();
    a["state"] = "idle".into();
    a
}

pub struct InboxRig {
    pub f: FakeServer,
    pub c: FakeConn,
    pub inbox: Arc<Inbox>,
}

pub async fn inbox_rig(agents: Vec<Value>) -> InboxRig {
    let f = FakeServer::start(TOKEN, Identity::Paired).await;
    let secrets = Arc::new(MemorySecrets::default());
    secrets
        .set("m1", &MachineSecret { token: TOKEN.into(), pin: f.pin() }.to_json())
        .unwrap();
    let settings = Settings {
        client_id: "00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001".into(),
        hotkey: DEFAULT_HOTKEY.into(),
        show_idle: false,
        show_subagents: false,
        machines: vec![MachineEntry {
            key: "m1".into(),
            name: "Box".into(),
            endpoint: EndpointConfig::Tcp { host: "127.0.0.1".into(), port: f.port() },
        }],
    };
    let inbox = Inbox::new(
        settings,
        secrets,
        InboxOptions { timing: fast(), settings_path: None, client_name: "t".into(), client_version: "0".into() },
    );
    inbox.start();
    let c = f.next_connection().await;
    let n = agents.len();
    c.send(json!({"type":"snapshot","epoch":"e1","seq":1,"agents":agents})).await;
    tokio::time::timeout(Duration::from_secs(5), async {
        loop {
            let s = inbox.stamped_view();
            if s.view.cards.len() + s.hidden_idle_cards.len() == n && !s.stamps.is_empty() {
                break;
            }
            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(10)).await;
        }
    })
    .await
    .expect("the inbox shows the agents");
    InboxRig { f, c, inbox }
}

pub async fn view_until(inbox: &Inbox, what: &str, pred: impl Fn(&InboxView) -> bool) {
    let mut rx = inbox.view();
    tokio::time::timeout(Duration::from_secs(5), async {
        loop {
            if pred(&rx.borrow()) {
                break;
            }
            rx.changed().await.unwrap();
        }
    })
    .await
    .unwrap_or_else(|_| panic!("timed out waiting for {what}"));
}

pub fn shown(selected: Option<&str>, visible: &[&str]) -> Presentation {
    Presentation {
        epoch: "e".into(),
        version: 1,
        selected: selected.map(str::to_owned),
        visible: visible.iter().map(|s| (*s).to_owned()).collect(),
        machine_filter: None,
        done_collapsed: true,
    }
}

pub fn frames(f: &FakeServer, kind: &str) -> Vec<Value> {
    f.received().into_iter().filter(|v| v["type"] == kind).collect()
}

pub async fn frame(f: &FakeServer, kind: &str) -> Value {
    tokio::time::timeout(Duration::from_secs(5), async {
        loop {
            if let Some(v) = frames(f, kind).into_iter().next() {
                return v;
            }
            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(10)).await;
        }
    })
    .await
    .unwrap_or_else(|_| panic!("no {kind} frame"))
}
```

Create `crates/acd-voice-client/tests/port.rs`:

```rust
mod common;

use acd_client::voice::{ActionOutcome, NotSentReason as ClientReason};
use acd_voice::plan::{Enqueue, EnqueueError, Guard, InboxPort, NotSentReason, VoiceOutcome};
use acd_voice::text::VoiceText;
use acd_voice_client::{AcdPort, freeze, voice_outcome};
use common::*;

#[tokio::test]
async fn the_port_enqueues_only_on_the_state_the_step_was_planned_on() {
    let r = inbox_rig(vec![pending_agent()]).await;
    let port = AcdPort::new(r.inbox.clone());
    let snap = freeze(&r.inbox.stamped_view(), shown(Some("m1/a1"), &["m1/a1"]), true);
    let card = snap.card("m1/a1").unwrap();
    let guard = Guard { conn: card.conn.unwrap(), agent_id: "a1".into(), request: Some(("r1".into(), 1)) };
    let stale = Guard { request: Some(("r1".into(), 9)), ..guard.clone() };
    let pick = |guard| Enqueue::Pick { card: "m1/a1".into(), option_id: "allow".into(), guard };
    assert_eq!(port.enqueue(pick(stale)), Err(EnqueueError::Stale));
    let id = port.enqueue(pick(guard)).unwrap();
    assert!(port.cancel(&id));
    assert!(!port.cancel(&id));
}

/// D7: a named send reaches an idle agent the panel hides; its card
/// shows while the countdown runs.
#[tokio::test]
async fn a_send_reaches_an_idle_agent_the_panel_hides() {
    let r = inbox_rig(vec![pending_agent(), idle_agent()]).await;
    let snap = freeze(&r.inbox.stamped_view(), shown(None, &["m1/a1"]), false);
    let idle = snap.card("m1/a3").expect("frozen although hidden");
    assert!(idle.idle_hidden);
    let guard = Guard { conn: idle.conn.unwrap(), agent_id: "a3".into(), request: None };
    let port = AcdPort::new(r.inbox.clone());
    port.enqueue(Enqueue::Reply { card: "m1/a3".into(), text: VoiceText::new("run the tests"), guard }).unwrap();
    let sent = frame(&r.f, "agent.send").await;
    assert_eq!((sent["agent_id"].as_str(), sent["text"].as_str()), (Some("a3"), Some("run the tests")));
}

#[test]
fn outcomes_map_one_to_one() {
    use acd_protocol::ErrorCode;
    for (client, voice) in [
        (ActionOutcome::Fired, VoiceOutcome::Fired),
        (ActionOutcome::Delivered, VoiceOutcome::Delivered),
        (ActionOutcome::Rejected { code: ErrorCode::RequestGone }, VoiceOutcome::Rejected { code: ErrorCode::RequestGone }),
        (ActionOutcome::Uncertain, VoiceOutcome::Uncertain),
        (ActionOutcome::Cancelled, VoiceOutcome::Cancelled),
        (ActionOutcome::NotSent { reason: ClientReason::Hidden }, VoiceOutcome::NotSent { reason: NotSentReason::Hidden }),
        (ActionOutcome::NotSent { reason: ClientReason::AnsweredElsewhere }, VoiceOutcome::NotSent { reason: NotSentReason::AnsweredElsewhere }),
    ] {
        assert_eq!(voice_outcome(client), voice);
    }
}
```

```rust
//! acd-client's stamped view, frozen into an acd-voice snapshot (spec §4.3).
use std::collections::HashMap;

use acd_client::view::{CardGroup, CardView, InboxView};
use acd_client::voice::{ConnStamp as ClientStamp, StampedView};
use acd_voice::snapshot::{CardSnap, ConnStamp, Group, OptionSnap, Presentation, RequestSnap, Snapshot};

/// The cards and their stamps come from one rebuild (D5).
pub fn freeze(stamped: &StampedView, presentation: Presentation, panel_open: bool) -> Snapshot {
    let stamp = |machine: &str| stamped.stamps.get(machine).copied().map(convert);
    let mut cards: Vec<CardSnap> = stamped
        .view
        .cards
        .iter()
        .map(|c| card(c, stamp(&c.machine_key), false))
        .collect();
    cards.extend(stamped.hidden_idle_cards.iter().map(|c| card(c, stamp(&c.machine_key), true)));
    Snapshot { presentation, panel_open, show_subagents: stamped.view.show_subagents, cards }
}

pub(crate) fn convert(s: ClientStamp) -> ConnStamp {
    ConnStamp { instance: s.instance, conn_gen: s.conn_gen }
}

fn group(g: CardGroup) -> Group {
    match g {
        CardGroup::Request => Group::Request,
        CardGroup::Done => Group::Done,
        CardGroup::Working => Group::Working,
        CardGroup::Unknown => Group::Unknown,
        CardGroup::Idle => Group::Idle,
    }
}

fn card(c: &CardView, conn: Option<ConnStamp>, idle_hidden: bool) -> CardSnap {
    CardSnap {
        key: c.key.clone(),
        machine_key: c.machine_key.clone(),
        machine_name: c.machine_name.clone(),
        online: c.live,
        conn,
        agent_id: c.agent.id.clone(),
        kind: c.agent.kind,
        title: c.agent.title.clone(),
        project: c.agent.project.clone(),
        state: c.agent.state,
        role: c.agent.role,
        group: group(c.group),
        can_reply: c.can_reply,
        pending: c.agent.pending.as_ref().map(|r| RequestSnap {
            id: r.id.clone(),
            revision: r.revision,
            kind: r.kind,
            title: r.title.clone(),
            detail: r.detail.clone(),
            question: r.questions.as_ref().and_then(|q| q.first()).map(|q| q.text.clone()),
            answerable: c.answerable,
            free_text: c.free_text_answerable,
            options: r
                .options
                .iter()
                .map(|o| OptionSnap {
                    id: o.id.clone(),
                    label: o.label.clone(),
                    description: o.description.clone(),
                    effect: o.effect,
                    selectable: c.selectable_options.contains(&o.id),
                })
                .collect(),
        }),
        idle_hidden,
    }
}
```

Create `crates/acd-voice-client/src/port.rs`:

```rust
//! `InboxPort` over acd-client's `Inbox`: one guarded delegate per
//! operation. acd-client checks the guard on the live machine state.
use std::sync::Arc;

use acd_client::inbox::{Inbox, PickError};
use acd_client::voice::{ActionOutcome, ConnStamp as ClientStamp, Guard as ClientGuard, NotSentReason as ClientReason};
use acd_voice::plan::{Enqueue, EnqueueError, Guard, InboxPort, NotSentReason, VoiceOutcome};

pub struct AcdPort {
    inbox: Arc<Inbox>,
}

impl AcdPort {
    pub fn new(inbox: Arc<Inbox>) -> Self {
        Self { inbox }
    }
}

impl InboxPort for AcdPort {
    fn enqueue(&self, op: Enqueue) -> Result<String, EnqueueError> {
        let result = match op {
            Enqueue::Pick { card, option_id, guard } => self.inbox.pick_guarded(&card, &option_id, &client(&guard)),
            Enqueue::AnswerText { card, text, guard } => {
                self.inbox.answer_text_guarded(&card, text.expose().to_owned(), &client(&guard))
            }
            Enqueue::Reply { card, text, guard } => {
                self.inbox.reply_guarded(&card, text.expose().to_owned(), &client(&guard))
            }
        };
        result.map_err(enqueue_error)
    }

    fn cancel(&self, action_id: &str) -> bool {
        self.inbox.cancel(action_id)
    }
}

fn client(g: &Guard) -> ClientGuard {
    ClientGuard {
        conn: ClientStamp { instance: g.conn.instance, conn_gen: g.conn.conn_gen },
        agent_id: g.agent_id.clone(),
        request: g.request.clone(),
    }
}

fn enqueue_error(e: PickError) -> EnqueueError {
    match e {
        PickError::UnknownCard => EnqueueError::UnknownCard,
        PickError::NotAnswerable => EnqueueError::NotAnswerable,
        PickError::StaleRequest | PickError::UnknownOption => EnqueueError::Stale,
        PickError::AlreadyCounting => EnqueueError::AlreadyCounting,
        PickError::BadText | PickError::BadAnswerText => EnqueueError::BadText,
        PickError::CannotReply => EnqueueError::CannotReply,
        PickError::ConnectionChanged => EnqueueError::ConnectionChanged,
    }
}

fn reason(r: ClientReason) -> NotSentReason {
    match r {
        ClientReason::Offline => NotSentReason::Offline,
        ClientReason::NotSupported => NotSentReason::NotSupported,
        ClientReason::AgentGone => NotSentReason::AgentGone,
        ClientReason::AnsweredElsewhere => NotSentReason::AnsweredElsewhere,
        ClientReason::Changed => NotSentReason::Changed,
        ClientReason::AgentBusy => NotSentReason::AgentBusy,
        ClientReason::Hidden => NotSentReason::Hidden,
    }
}

pub fn voice_outcome(o: ActionOutcome) -> VoiceOutcome {
    match o {
        ActionOutcome::Fired => VoiceOutcome::Fired,
        ActionOutcome::Delivered => VoiceOutcome::Delivered,
        ActionOutcome::Rejected { code } => VoiceOutcome::Rejected { code },
        ActionOutcome::Uncertain => VoiceOutcome::Uncertain,
        ActionOutcome::Cancelled => VoiceOutcome::Cancelled,
        ActionOutcome::NotSent { reason: r } => VoiceOutcome::NotSent { reason: reason(r) },
    }
}
```

### Task 11: `acd-voice-client`: controller lifecycle

Depends on Task 10.

**Files:**
- Create: `crates/acd-voice-client/src/controller.rs`
- Modify: `crates/acd-voice-client/src/lib.rs`, `crates/acd-voice-client/tests/common/mod.rs`
- Create: `crates/acd-voice-client/tests/controller.rs`, `crates/acd-voice-client/tests/logs.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 10; `Executor` and `Hud` from Task 8.
- Produces (in `acd_voice_client`):
  - `trait HudSink: Send + Sync { fn hud(&self, seq: u64, hud: &Hud) -> bool; fn select(&self, card_key: &str); }`. `hud` returns false when the state could not be sent; a step waiting for display then fails closed.
  - `InputError` with variants `Empty` and `Off`.
  - `DISPLAY_TIMEOUT = 1 s`.
  - `Controller`, with these methods:
    - `start(Arc<Inbox>, Option<Arc<dyn IntentModel>>, Arc<dyn HudSink>, enabled: bool) -> Arc<Controller>`, which must be called inside a tokio runtime;
    - `publish(Presentation) -> bool`;
    - `async text_input(String, panel_open) -> Result<(), InputError>`;
    - `shown(proposal_id)`, `confirm(proposal_id)`, `choose(usize)` and `cancel()`;
    - `set_enabled(bool)`;
    - `on_admission_for_test(impl Fn())`, a hidden test hook.

Lifecycle rules:

1. **Ordered publication (B3).** Every executor call and the publication of its reaction happen under the executor lock. `seq` grows by one per publication, so the HUD can drop out-of-order events. Sinks must not call back into the controller. The app's sink only emits events.
2. **Display acknowledgement (B3, D22).** A reaction that awaits display starts a `DISPLAY_TIMEOUT` timer on the runtime handle captured at `start`. If the sink refuses the state, or the timer ends before `shown(id)`, `display_failed(id)` runs and nothing is sent.
3. **Sessions (B4).**
   - The executor, the enabled flag and the session generation live behind one lock (`Core`). `set_enabled` and every admission take it, so an input is either admitted before a reset, and dropped by it, or refused after it.
   - `set_enabled` bumps the session generation, which lives outside the resettable executor.
   - An utterance being routed is dropped as soon as the generation changes. `tokio::select!` drops the route future, which cancels the model call.
   - A result from an older generation is discarded.
   - Turning voice off also resets the executor, which cancels unsent work and clears voice text.
4. **Lag.** A lagged outcome stream runs `outcomes_lost`. That cancels a counting action, or marks the action uncertain if the cancel is too late or it already fired.

Append to `crates/acd-voice-client/tests/common/mod.rs`:

```rust
use std::sync::Mutex;
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering};

use acd_voice::hud::Hud;
use acd_voice::router::model::IntentModel;
use acd_voice_client::{Controller, HudSink};
use tokio::sync::mpsc;

/// Records what the controller publishes. With `acks`, it plays the HUD
/// webview: every state awaiting display is acknowledged, after the
/// publish returns (never from inside it).
#[derive(Default)]
pub struct Sink {
    pub huds: Mutex<Vec<(u64, Hud)>>,
    pub selects: Mutex<Vec<String>>,
    acks: Mutex<Option<mpsc::UnboundedSender<u64>>>,
    /// Refuse every state that awaits display, as a failed emit would.
    pub refuse_display: AtomicBool,
}

impl HudSink for Sink {
    fn hud(&self, seq: u64, hud: &Hud) -> bool {
        if hud.awaiting_display().is_some() && self.refuse_display.load(Ordering::SeqCst) {
            return false;
        }
        self.huds.lock().unwrap().push((seq, hud.clone()));
        if let (Some(id), Some(tx)) = (hud.awaiting_display(), self.acks.lock().unwrap().as_ref()) {
            let _ = tx.send(id);
        }
        true
    }
    fn select(&self, card_key: &str) {
        self.selects.lock().unwrap().push(card_key.to_owned());
    }
}

impl Sink {
    pub fn last(&self) -> Option<Hud> {
        self.huds.lock().unwrap().last().map(|(_, h)| h.clone())
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Display {
    /// The HUD acknowledges everything it is shown.
    Acks,
    /// The HUD never answers.
    Silent,
}

pub struct Rig {
    pub f: acd_client::fake::FakeServer,
    pub c: acd_client::fake::FakeConn,
    pub inbox: Arc<acd_client::inbox::Inbox>,
    pub voice: Arc<Controller>,
    pub sink: Arc<Sink>,
}

pub async fn rig(agents: Vec<Value>, model: Option<Arc<dyn IntentModel>>, display: Display) -> Rig {
    let InboxRig { f, c, inbox } = inbox_rig(agents).await;
    let sink = Arc::new(Sink::default());
    let voice = Controller::start(inbox.clone(), model, sink.clone(), true);
    if display == Display::Acks {
        let (tx, mut rx) = mpsc::unbounded_channel();
        *sink.acks.lock().unwrap() = Some(tx);
        let weak = Arc::downgrade(&voice);
        tokio::spawn(async move {
            while let Some(id) = rx.recv().await {
                let Some(voice) = weak.upgrade() else { return };
                voice.shown(id);
            }
        });
    }
    Rig { f, c, inbox, voice, sink }
}

pub async fn hud_until(sink: &Sink, what: &str, pred: impl Fn(&Hud) -> bool) -> Hud {
    tokio::time::timeout(Duration::from_secs(5), async {
        loop {
            if let Some(h) = sink.last().filter(|h| pred(h)) {
                return h;
            }
            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(10)).await;
        }
    })
    .await
    .unwrap_or_else(|_| panic!("timed out waiting for {what}"))
}
```

If `Controller::start(inbox.clone(), model, sink.clone(), true)` does not coerce `Arc<Sink>` to `Arc<dyn HudSink>`, write `sink.clone() as Arc<dyn HudSink>`.

```rust
mod common;

use std::sync::Arc;
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering};
use std::time::Duration;

use acd_protocol::ErrorCode;
use acd_voice::hud::{Hud, OutcomeView};
use acd_voice::refusal::Refusal;
use acd_voice::router::model::{FakeIntentModel, IntentModel, ModelError};
use acd_voice::router::prompt::Prompt;
use acd_voice::text::VoiceText;
use acd_voice_client::{DISPLAY_TIMEOUT, InputError};
use common::*;
use serde_json::json;

fn countdown_started(h: &Hud) -> bool {
    matches!(h, Hud::Countdown { action_id: Some(_), .. })
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn approve_counts_down_after_the_hud_showed_it_then_reports_delivery() {
    let r = rig(vec![pending_agent()], None, Display::Acks).await;
    r.voice.publish(shown(Some("m1/a1"), &["m1/a1"]));
    r.voice.text_input("approve".into(), true).await.unwrap();
    hud_until(&r.sink, "countdown", countdown_started).await;
    let sent = frame(&r.f, "request.answer").await;
    assert_eq!((sent["request_id"].as_str(), sent["option_id"].as_str()), (Some("r1"), Some("allow")));
    r.c.send(json!({"type":"result","id":sent["id"],"ok":true,"outcome":"delivered"})).await;
    hud_until(&r.sink, "delivered", |h| matches!(h, Hud::Done { outcome: OutcomeView::Delivered, .. })).await;
    let huds = r.sink.huds.lock().unwrap().clone();
    assert!(huds.windows(2).all(|w| w[0].0 < w[1].0), "published in order");
    let shown_at = huds.iter().position(|(_, h)| matches!(h, Hud::Countdown { action_id: None, .. })).unwrap();
    let started_at = huds.iter().position(|(_, h)| countdown_started(h)).unwrap();
    assert!(shown_at < started_at, "the step was on screen before its countdown started");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_hud_that_never_answers_sends_nothing() {
    let r = rig(vec![pending_agent()], None, Display::Silent).await;
    r.voice.publish(shown(Some("m1/a1"), &["m1/a1"]));
    r.voice.text_input("approve".into(), true).await.unwrap();
    tokio::time::sleep(DISPLAY_TIMEOUT + Duration::from_millis(300)).await;
    assert_eq!(r.sink.last(), Some(Hud::Error { refusal: Refusal::DisplayFailed, transcript: None }));
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(300)).await;
    assert!(frames(&r.f, "request.answer").is_empty());
}

    let r = rig(vec![pending_agent()], None, Display::Acks).await;
    r.sink.refuse_display.store(true, Ordering::SeqCst);
    r.voice.publish(shown(Some("m1/a1"), &["m1/a1"]));
    r.voice.text_input("approve".into(), true).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(r.sink.last(), Some(Hud::Error { refusal: Refusal::DisplayFailed, transcript: None }));
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(400)).await;

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_rejection_is_reported_with_its_code() {

async fn confirm_shown(r: &Rig) {
    let Hud::Confirm { proposal_id, .. } = hud_until(&r.sink, "confirm", |h| matches!(h, Hud::Confirm { .. })).await else {
        unreachable!()
    };
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(50)).await; // let the ack land
    r.voice.confirm(proposal_id);
}

#[tokio::test]

        Arc::new(FakeIntentModel::replying(r#"{"step":"send","agent":"a1","text":"run the tests"}"#));
    let r = rig(vec![working_agent()], Some(model), Display::Acks).await;
    r.voice.publish(shown(None, &["m1/a2"]));

/// D7: a named send reaches an idle agent the panel hides, confirmed first.
#[tokio::test]
async fn a_named_send_reaches_a_hidden_idle_agent() {
    // The prompt lists the visible a1 first, then the hidden idle a3 as "a2".
    let model: Arc<dyn IntentModel> =
        Arc::new(FakeIntentModel::replying(r#"{"step":"send","agent":"a2","text":"rebase"}"#));
    let r = rig(vec![pending_agent(), idle_agent()], Some(model), Display::Acks).await;
    r.voice.publish(shown(None, &["m1/a1"]));
    r.voice.text_input("tell the codex to rebase".into(), false).await.unwrap();
    let Hud::Confirm { proposal, .. } = hud_until(&r.sink, "confirm", |h| matches!(h, Hud::Confirm { .. })).await else {
        unreachable!()
    };
    assert!(proposal.target.off_screen);
    confirm_shown(&r).await;
    let sent = frame(&r.f, "agent.send").await;
    assert_eq!((sent["agent_id"].as_str(), sent["text"].as_str()), (Some("a3"), Some("rebase")));
}

/// A model that waits until released, and notes when its call is dropped.
struct Gated {
    gate: Arc<tokio::sync::Notify>,
    dropped: Arc<AtomicBool>,
}

#[async_trait::async_trait]
impl IntentModel for Gated {
    async fn plan(&self, _prompt: &Prompt) -> Result<VoiceText, ModelError> {
        struct OnDrop(Arc<AtomicBool>);
        impl Drop for OnDrop {
            fn drop(&mut self) {
                self.0.store(true, Ordering::SeqCst);
            }
        }
        let armed = OnDrop(self.dropped.clone());
        self.gate.notified().await;
        std::mem::forget(armed);
        Ok(VoiceText::new(r#"{"step":"send","agent":"a1","text":"old"}"#))
    }
}

/// B4: disable, enable, a new command; the old model call was cancelled
/// and its answer never lands.
#[tokio::test]
async fn turning_voice_off_and_on_drops_work_from_before() {
    let gate = Arc::new(tokio::sync::Notify::new());
    let dropped = Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(false));
    let model: Arc<dyn IntentModel> = Arc::new(Gated { gate: gate.clone(), dropped: dropped.clone() });
    let r = rig(vec![pending_agent(), working_agent()], Some(model), Display::Acks).await;
    r.voice.publish(shown(Some("m1/a1"), &["m1/a1", "m1/a2"]));
    let voice = r.voice.clone();
    let old = tokio::spawn(async move { voice.text_input("tell the box claude something".into(), true).await });
    hud_until(&r.sink, "thinking", |h| matches!(h, Hud::Thinking { .. })).await;
    r.voice.set_enabled(false);
    assert_eq!(r.voice.text_input("approve".into(), true).await, Err(InputError::Off));
    r.voice.set_enabled(true);
    assert_eq!(old.await.unwrap(), Ok(()));
    assert!(dropped.load(Ordering::SeqCst), "the model call was cancelled");
    r.voice.text_input("approve".into(), true).await.unwrap();
    hud_until(&r.sink, "countdown", countdown_started).await;
    gate.notify_waiters();
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(300)).await;
    assert!(!r.sink.huds.lock().unwrap().iter().any(|(_, h)| matches!(h, Hud::Confirm { .. })), "the old answer never lands");
    assert!(frames(&r.f, "agent.send").is_empty());
}

/// A model that only counts its calls.
struct Counting(Arc<std::sync::atomic::AtomicUsize>);

#[async_trait::async_trait]
impl IntentModel for Counting {
    async fn plan(&self, _prompt: &Prompt) -> Result<VoiceText, ModelError> {
        self.0.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst);
        Ok(VoiceText::new(r#"{"step":"none"}"#))
    }
}

/// B4, the interleaving itself: the input has arrived, voice turns off
/// before admission. Admission and reset share one lock, so the input is
/// refused and no work starts while voice is off. (The other order, a
/// reset after admission, is `turning_voice_off_and_on_drops_work_from_before`.)
#[tokio::test]
async fn a_reset_racing_admission_never_starts_work_while_disabled() {
    let calls = Arc::new(std::sync::atomic::AtomicUsize::new(0));
    let model: Arc<dyn IntentModel> = Arc::new(Counting(calls.clone()));
    let r = rig(vec![pending_agent()], Some(model), Display::Acks).await;
    let weak = Arc::downgrade(&r.voice);
    r.voice.on_admission_for_test(move || {
        if let Some(voice) = weak.upgrade() {
            voice.set_enabled(false);
        }
    });
    assert_eq!(r.voice.text_input("tell the box claude to go on".into(), true).await, Err(InputError::Off));
    assert_eq!(calls.load(Ordering::SeqCst), 0, "no model work started");
    assert!(
        !r.sink.huds.lock().unwrap().iter().any(|(_, h)| matches!(h, Hud::Thinking { .. })),
        "nothing was admitted"
    );
    assert_eq!(r.sink.last(), Some(Hud::Hidden));
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn turning_voice_off_cancels_and_hides() {
    let r = rig(vec![pending_agent()], None, Display::Acks).await;
    r.voice.publish(shown(Some("m1/a1"), &["m1/a1"]));
    r.voice.text_input("approve".into(), true).await.unwrap();
    hud_until(&r.sink, "countdown", countdown_started).await;
    r.voice.set_enabled(false);
    assert_eq!(r.sink.last(), Some(Hud::Hidden));
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(400)).await;
    assert!(frames(&r.f, "request.answer").is_empty());
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn stale_presentations_lose_and_an_empty_command_is_refused() {
    let r = rig(vec![pending_agent()], None, Display::Acks).await;
    let mut newer = shown(Some("m1/a1"), &["m1/a1"]);
    newer.version = 2;
    assert!(r.voice.publish(newer));
    assert!(!r.voice.publish(shown(None, &[])));
    assert_eq!(r.voice.text_input("  ".into(), true).await, Err(InputError::Empty));
}
```

```rust
mod common;

use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
use std::time::Duration;

use acd_voice::hud::Hud;
use acd_voice::router::model::{FakeIntentModel, IntentModel};
use common::*;
use serde_json::json;

#[derive(Clone, Default)]
struct Buffer(Arc<Mutex<Vec<u8>>>);

impl std::io::Write for Buffer {
    fn write(&mut self, bytes: &[u8]) -> std::io::Result<usize> {
        self.0.lock().unwrap().extend_from_slice(bytes);
        Ok(bytes.len())
    }
    fn flush(&mut self) -> std::io::Result<()> {
        Ok(())
    }
}

/// Spec §3.7, through the real client transport: transcript, payload,
/// daemon error messages and result messages never reach the logs.
/// `set_default` is per thread, so the runtime is current-thread.
#[tokio::test(flavor = "current_thread")]
async fn voice_text_never_reaches_the_logs() {
    let buffer = Buffer::default();
    let writer = buffer.clone();
    let subscriber = tracing_subscriber::fmt()
        .with_max_level(tracing::Level::TRACE)
        .with_ansi(false)
        .with_writer(move || writer.clone())
        .finish();
    let _guard = tracing::subscriber::set_default(subscriber);

    let model: Arc<dyn IntentModel> = Arc::new(FakeIntentModel::replying(
        r#"{"step":"send","agent":"a1","text":"SECRET-PAYLOAD-7f3a"}"#,
    ));
    let r = rig(vec![working_agent()], Some(model), Display::Acks).await;
    r.voice.publish(shown(None, &["m1/a2"]));
    r.voice
        .text_input("tell the box claude SECRET-TRANSCRIPT-91c2 SECRET-PAYLOAD-7f3a".into(), false)
        .await
        .unwrap();
    let Hud::Confirm { proposal_id, .. } = hud_until(&r.sink, "confirm", |h| matches!(h, Hud::Confirm { .. })).await else {
        unreachable!()
    };
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(50)).await;
    r.voice.confirm(proposal_id);
    let sent = frame(&r.f, "agent.send").await;
    r.c.send(json!({"type":"error","code":"bad_request","message":"SECRET-DAEMON echo"})).await;
    r.c.send(json!({"type":"result","id":sent["id"],"ok":false,"error":{"code":"agent_busy","message":"SECRET-RESULT"}})).await;
    hud_until(&r.sink, "rejected", |h| matches!(h, Hud::Done { .. })).await;
    r.voice.text_input("SECRET-TRANSCRIPT-91c2 what now".into(), false).await.unwrap();

    let logs = String::from_utf8(buffer.0.lock().unwrap().clone()).unwrap();
    assert!(logs.contains("utterance routed"), "the capture works: {logs}");
    assert!(logs.contains("voice action outcome"), "{logs}");
    assert!(logs.contains("daemon error"), "{logs}");
    assert!(!logs.contains("SECRET"), "{logs}");
}
```

Create `crates/acd-voice-client/src/controller.rs`:

```rust
//! One utterance at a time: freeze, route, show, execute, report.
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering};
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex, Weak};
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

use acd_client::inbox::Inbox;
use acd_client::voice::{ActionEvent, ActionOutcome};
use acd_voice::hud::Hud;
use acd_voice::plan::{Executor, Reaction};
use acd_voice::router::model::IntentModel;
use acd_voice::router::{RouteOptions, route};
use acd_voice::snapshot::{Presentation, Snapshot};
use acd_voice::text::VoiceText;
use tokio::sync::{broadcast, watch};

use crate::freeze::freeze;
use crate::port::{AcdPort, voice_outcome};

/// How long the HUD has to acknowledge a step before it fails closed (D22).
pub const DISPLAY_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(1);

/// Where the controller shows things; the app emits Tauri events. It must
/// not call back into the controller: it runs under the executor lock.
pub trait HudSink: Send + Sync {
    /// Publishes `hud` as number `seq`; false if it could not be sent.
    fn hud(&self, seq: u64, hud: &Hud) -> bool;
    fn select(&self, card_key: &str);
}

#[derive(Debug, PartialEq, Eq, thiserror::Error)]
pub enum InputError {
    #[error("The command is empty")]
    Empty,
    #[error("Voice is off. Turn it on in Settings.")]
    Off,
}

/// Everything admission depends on, behind one lock (B4): the executor,
/// whether voice is on, and the session generation. `set_enabled` and
/// every admission take this lock, so an utterance is either admitted
/// before a reset (and dropped by it) or refused after it. There is no
/// third order.
struct Core {
    exec: Executor,
    enabled: bool,
    session: u64,
}

pub struct Controller {
    me: Weak<Controller>,
    runtime: tokio::runtime::Handle,
    inbox: Arc<Inbox>,
    port: AcdPort,
    core: Mutex<Core>,
    presentation: Mutex<Option<Presentation>>,
    model: Option<Arc<dyn IntentModel>>,
    sink: Arc<dyn HudSink>,
    opts: RouteOptions,
    /// Mirrors `Core::session` for `select!`; written only under the core
    /// lock, so a receiver made under that lock sees every later change.
    session_tx: watch::Sender<u64>,
    seq: AtomicU64,
    /// Test hook: runs with no lock held, just before admission.
    admission_hook: Mutex<Option<Arc<dyn Fn() + Send + Sync>>>,
}

impl Controller {
    /// Subscribes to action outcomes before anything can be enqueued, then
    /// forwards them. Call inside a tokio runtime.
    pub fn start(
        inbox: Arc<Inbox>,
        model: Option<Arc<dyn IntentModel>>,
        sink: Arc<dyn HudSink>,
        enabled: bool,
    ) -> Arc<Self> {
        let outcomes = inbox.action_outcomes();
        let this = Arc::new_cyclic(|me| Self {
            me: me.clone(),
            runtime: tokio::runtime::Handle::current(),
            port: AcdPort::new(inbox.clone()),
            inbox,
            core: Mutex::new(Core { exec: Executor::new(), enabled, session: 0 }),
            presentation: Mutex::new(None),
            model,
            sink,
            opts: RouteOptions::default(),
            session_tx: watch::channel(0).0,
            seq: AtomicU64::new(0),
            admission_hook: Mutex::new(None),
        });
        this.runtime.spawn(forward_outcomes(Arc::downgrade(&this), outcomes));
        this
    }

    /// The panel's latest presentation (spec §4.3); stale publishes lose.
    pub fn publish(&self, presentation: Presentation) -> bool {
        let mut current = self.presentation.lock().unwrap();
        if !presentation.supersedes(current.as_ref()) {
            return false;
        }
        *current = Some(presentation);
        true
    }

    /// Voice on or off. Either way a new session starts, under the same
    /// lock as admission (B4). Off also cancels unsent work, hides the HUD
    /// and drops voice text from memory (spec §6.3).
    pub fn set_enabled(&self, on: bool) {
        let mut core = self.core.lock().unwrap();
        core.enabled = on;
        core.session += 1;
        self.session_tx.send_replace(core.session);
        if !on {
            core.exec.reset(&self.port);
            self.emit(&mut core.exec, Reaction { hud: Hud::Hidden, select: None });
        }
    }

    /// Test hook: `hook` runs just before admission, with no lock held, so
    /// a test can force "voice turned off after the input arrived but
    /// before it was admitted".
    #[doc(hidden)]
    pub fn on_admission_for_test(&self, hook: impl Fn() + Send + Sync + 'static) {
        *self.admission_hook.lock().unwrap() = Some(Arc::new(hook));
    }

    /// The step-1 stand-in for a recording: typed text as the transcript.
    pub async fn text_input(&self, text: String, panel_open: bool) -> Result<(), InputError> {
        if text.trim().is_empty() {
            return Err(InputError::Empty);
        }
        let started = Instant::now();
        let transcript = VoiceText::new(text);
        let snapshot = self.freeze(panel_open);
        let hook = self.admission_hook.lock().unwrap().clone();
        if let Some(hook) = hook {
            hook();
        }
        // Admission (B4): the enabled check, the session, and reply-or-begin
        // happen under one lock, the one `set_enabled` takes.
        let admitted = {
            let mut core = self.core.lock().unwrap();
            if !core.enabled {
                return Err(InputError::Off);
            }
            let session = core.session;
            let changes = self.session_tx.subscribe();
            // A HUD that waits for input takes the utterance as its answer (D21).
            if let Some(r) = core.exec.reply(&self.port, &transcript, self.opts.locale) {
                self.emit(&mut core.exec, r);
                None
            } else {
                let (n, thinking) = core.exec.begin(&self.port, snapshot.clone(), transcript.clone());
                self.emit(&mut core.exec, thinking);
                Some((session, n, changes))
            }
        };
        let Some((session, n, mut changes)) = admitted else {
            tracing::info!(target: "acd_voice", chars = transcript.char_len(), route = "reply", "utterance routed");
            return Ok(());
        };
        let routed = tokio::select! {
            routed = route(&snapshot, &transcript, self.model.as_deref(), &self.opts) => routed,
            // Voice was turned off or on: drop the work, cancelling any model call (B4).
            _ = changes.changed() => return Ok(()),
        };
        let mut core = self.core.lock().unwrap();
        if core.session != session || !core.enabled {
            return Ok(());
        }
        tracing::info!(
            target: "acd_voice",
            chars = transcript.char_len(),
            route = routed.tag(),
            elapsed_ms = started.elapsed().as_millis() as u64,
            "utterance routed"
        );
        if let Some(r) = core.exec.routed(n, routed) {
            self.emit(&mut core.exec, r);
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    /// The HUD rendered proposal `id` (D22).
    pub fn shown(&self, id: u64) {
        self.act(|exec, port| exec.shown(port, id));
    }

    /// Enter or a click on the HUD's Confirm for proposal `id`.
    pub fn confirm(&self, id: u64) {
        self.act(|exec, port| exec.confirm(port, Some(id)));
    }

    pub fn choose(&self, index: usize) {
        self.act(|exec, _| exec.choose(index));
    }

    pub fn cancel(&self) {
        self.act(|exec, port| exec.cancel(port));
    }

    /// Runs `f` on the executor and publishes its reaction while still
    /// holding the core lock, so states reach the HUD in the order they
    /// happened (B3).
    fn act(&self, f: impl FnOnce(&mut Executor, &AcdPort) -> Option<Reaction>) {
        let mut core = self.core.lock().unwrap();
        if let Some(reaction) = f(&mut core.exec, &self.port) {
            self.emit(&mut core.exec, reaction);
        }
    }

    fn emit(&self, exec: &mut Executor, mut reaction: Reaction) {
        loop {
            if let Some(card) = &reaction.select {
                self.sink.select(card);
            }
            let seq = self.seq.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst) + 1;
            let delivered = self.sink.hud(seq, &reaction.hud);
            let Some(id) = reaction.hud.awaiting_display() else {
                return;
            };
            if delivered {
                self.watch_display(id);
                return;
            }
            // The HUD cannot show it: fail closed (D22).
            match exec.display_failed(id) {
                Some(r) => reaction = r,
                None => return,
            }
        }
    }

    fn watch_display(&self, id: u64) {
        let me = self.me.clone();
        self.runtime.spawn(async move {
            tokio::time::sleep(DISPLAY_TIMEOUT).await;
            if let Some(this) = me.upgrade() {
                this.act(|exec, _| exec.display_failed(id));
            }
        });
    }

    fn freeze(&self, panel_open: bool) -> Snapshot {
        let presentation = self.presentation.lock().unwrap().clone().unwrap_or_default();
        freeze(&self.inbox.stamped_view(), presentation, panel_open)
    }
}

fn outcome_tag(o: ActionOutcome) -> &'static str {
    match o {
        ActionOutcome::Fired => "fired",
        ActionOutcome::Delivered => "delivered",
        ActionOutcome::Rejected { .. } => "rejected",
        ActionOutcome::Uncertain => "uncertain",
        ActionOutcome::Cancelled => "cancelled",
        ActionOutcome::NotSent { .. } => "not_sent",
    }
}

async fn forward_outcomes(this: Weak<Controller>, mut rx: broadcast::Receiver<ActionEvent>) {
    loop {
        let event = rx.recv().await;
        let Some(this) = this.upgrade() else {
            return;
        };
        match event {
            Ok(e) => this.act(|exec, _| {
                let r = exec.outcome(&e.action_id, voice_outcome(e.outcome));
                if r.is_some() {
                    tracing::info!(target: "acd_voice", outcome = outcome_tag(e.outcome), "voice action outcome");
                }
                r
            }),
            Err(broadcast::error::RecvError::Lagged(_)) => this.act(|exec, port| exec.outcomes_lost(port)),
            Err(broadcast::error::RecvError::Closed) => return,
        }
    }
}
```

Depends on Task 11. This is macOS only: `app/src-tauri` builds only on macOS. On Linux, report `npm run verify` as pending for CI.

**Files:**
- Modify: `app/src-tauri/Cargo.toml`, `app/src-tauri/Cargo.lock`
- Create: `app/src-tauri/src/voice/mod.rs`, `voice/settings.rs`, `voice/commands.rs`, and `voice/hud.rs` (event publishing only; Task 13 adds the panel)
- Modify: `app/src-tauri/src/main.rs`: `mod voice;`, the `invoke_handler` list, and the controller in `setup`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `acd_voice_client::{Controller, HudSink}`, `acd_voice::hud::{Hud, HudEvent}`, `acd_voice::snapshot::Presentation`.
- Produces: Tauri commands, called from TypeScript with camelCase argument names:

  | Command | Arguments | Returns |
  |---|---|---|
  | `voice_get_settings` | none | `VoiceSettings` |
  | `voice_set_settings` | `{ enabled, debugInput }` | `VoiceSettings` |
  | `voice_publish_presentation` | `{ presentation }` | nothing |
  | `voice_text_input` | `{ text }` | nothing; errors are static strings |
  | `voice_shown` | `{ proposalId }` | nothing |
  | `voice_confirm` | `{ proposalId }` | nothing |
  | `voice_choose` | `{ index }` | nothing |
  | `voice_cancel` | none | nothing |

- Produces events:
  - `voice://hud`, a `HudEvent { seq, hud }`;
  - `voice://settings`, a `VoiceSettings`;
  - `voice://select`, a card key.

  All are broadcast like `inbox://view`; only the intended window listens.
- `VoiceSettings { enabled: bool, debug_input: bool }`, snake_case, both false by default (D12).

Until Task 13 there is no HUD window. Nothing acknowledges a step, so every step fails closed with `display_failed`. That is the intended behaviour without a HUD.

```rust
//! Voice switches (voice spec §8, the step-1 subset), in `voice.json` next
//! to `settings.json` (D12). Only switches live here, never voice content.
use std::fs;
use std::io::Write;
use std::os::unix::fs::{OpenOptionsExt, PermissionsExt};
use std::path::Path;

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct VoiceSettings {
    /// Off by default (spec §8).
    #[serde(default)]
    pub enabled: bool,
    /// Show the typed command field in the inbox panel (D13).
    #[serde(default)]
    pub debug_input: bool,
}

impl VoiceSettings {
    /// A missing or unreadable file means the defaults: voice off.
    pub fn load(path: &Path) -> Self {
        fs::read_to_string(path)
            .ok()
            .and_then(|raw| serde_json::from_str(&raw).ok())
            .unwrap_or_default()
    }

    /// Atomic write, mode 0600, parent directory 0700 (as `Settings::save`).
    pub fn save(&self, path: &Path) -> std::io::Result<()> {
        if let Some(dir) = path.parent() {
            fs::create_dir_all(dir)?;
            fs::set_permissions(dir, fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o700))?;
        }
        let tmp = path.with_extension("json.tmp");
        let _ = fs::remove_file(&tmp);
        let mut f = fs::OpenOptions::new().write(true).create_new(true).mode(0o600).open(&tmp)?;
        f.write_all(serde_json::to_string_pretty(self).expect("serializable").as_bytes())?;
        f.sync_all()?;
        fs::rename(&tmp, path)
    }
}
```

Create `app/src-tauri/src/voice/hud.rs`. Task 13 replaces it with the native panel:

```rust
//! The voice HUD's state, published to the HUD webview (spec §4.4).
use acd_voice::hud::{Hud, HudEvent};
use tauri::{AppHandle, Emitter};

/// Broadcast like `inbox://view`; only the HUD webview listens. False if
/// the event could not be sent: a step waiting for display then fails
/// closed (D22).
pub fn present(app: &AppHandle, seq: u64, hud: &Hud) -> bool {
    match app.emit("voice://hud", HudEvent { seq, hud }) {
        Ok(()) => true,
        Err(error) => {
            tracing::error!(%error, "could not emit the HUD state");
            false
        }
    }
}
```

Create `app/src-tauri/src/voice/mod.rs`:

```rust
//! Voice step 1 (voice spec §11 item 1): the typed command that stands in
//! for audio, the HUD, and the presentation snapshot.
pub mod commands;
pub mod hud;
pub mod settings;

use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};

use acd_voice::hud::Hud;
use acd_voice_client::{Controller, HudSink};
use tauri::{AppHandle, Emitter};

pub struct VoiceState {
    pub controller: Arc<Controller>,
    pub settings: Mutex<settings::VoiceSettings>,
    pub path: Option<PathBuf>,
}

/// HUD states go to the HUD webview; selections go to the inbox panel.
/// It only emits: it runs under the controller's executor lock.
pub struct TauriSink {
    app: AppHandle,
}

impl TauriSink {
    pub fn new(app: AppHandle) -> Self {
        Self { app }
    }
}

impl HudSink for TauriSink {
    fn hud(&self, seq: u64, state: &Hud) -> bool {
        hud::present(&self.app, seq, state)
    }

    fn select(&self, card_key: &str) {
        crate::panel::show(&self.app);
        // Broadcast; only the inbox panel listens.
        if let Err(error) = self.app.emit("voice://select", card_key) {
            tracing::error!(%error, "could not emit the voice selection");
        }
    }
}
```

Create `app/src-tauri/src/voice/commands.rs`:

```rust
use acd_voice::snapshot::Presentation;
use tauri::{AppHandle, Emitter, State};

use super::VoiceState;
use super::settings::VoiceSettings;

#[tauri::command]
pub fn voice_get_settings(voice: State<'_, VoiceState>) -> VoiceSettings {
    *voice.settings.lock().unwrap()
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn voice_set_settings(
    app: AppHandle,
    voice: State<'_, VoiceState>,
    enabled: bool,
    debug_input: bool,
) -> Result<VoiceSettings, String> {
    let next = VoiceSettings { enabled, debug_input };
    if let Some(path) = &voice.path {
        next.save(path).map_err(|_| "Could not save the voice settings".to_owned())?;
    }
    *voice.settings.lock().unwrap() = next;
    voice.controller.set_enabled(enabled);
    if let Err(error) = app.emit("voice://settings", next) {
        tracing::error!(%error, "could not emit the voice settings");
    }
    Ok(next)
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn voice_publish_presentation(voice: State<'_, VoiceState>, presentation: Presentation) {
    voice.controller.publish(presentation);
}

/// The step-1 trigger: typed text routed as a transcript. Errors are
/// static strings; the text never appears in one.
#[tauri::command]
pub async fn voice_text_input(voice: State<'_, VoiceState>, text: String) -> Result<(), String> {
    let controller = voice.controller.clone();
    controller
        .text_input(text, crate::panel::is_visible())
        .await
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())
}

/// The HUD rendered proposal `proposal_id` (D22).
#[tauri::command]
pub fn voice_shown(voice: State<'_, VoiceState>, proposal_id: u64) {
    voice.controller.shown(proposal_id);
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn voice_confirm(voice: State<'_, VoiceState>, proposal_id: u64) {
    voice.controller.confirm(proposal_id);
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn voice_choose(voice: State<'_, VoiceState>, index: usize) {
    voice.controller.choose(index);
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn voice_cancel(voice: State<'_, VoiceState>) {
    voice.controller.cancel();
}
```

### Task 13: App backend: native HUD panel and focus handoff

Depends on Task 12. This is macOS only.

**Files:**
- Modify: `app/src-tauri/src/voice/hud.rs`, which is replaced
- Modify: `app/src-tauri/src/main.rs`: `voice::hud::create` after `panel::create`
- Modify: `app/src-tauri/src/panel.rs`:
  - the resign-key handler (around 87–91);
  - `screen_frames` and `fallback_screen` become `pub(crate)`
- Modify: `app/src-tauri/capabilities/main.json`: add the `hud` window
- Modify: `app/src/App.svelte`, `app/src/App.test.ts`: the `hud` label never renders the panel

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 12.
- Produces:
  - `voice::hud::create`, `present(app, seq, &Hud) -> bool`, `place_on`, `fade_delay`, `holds_key`, `LABEL`, `SIZE` and `FADE_AFTER`.
  - The event `voice://hud-clear(seq)`. When a fade ends with no newer state, the HUD webview clears that state and the panel hides.

Rules:

- **Panel order (D22).** `present` runs one main-thread closure. It presents the panel, checks `is_visible()`, and only then emits the state. If the panel did not appear, nothing is emitted and the controller's display timeout fails the step closed. The webview's acknowledgement therefore always follows a panel that is on screen.
- **Key handoff (D17).** `HANDOFF` is an intentional handoff flag. `apply` sets it only when it makes the HUD key for confirm, which-one or countdown. It is cleared by `release_key`, or by the HUD resigning key on its own when the user clicks elsewhere. While it is set, the inbox panel does not hide on resigning key. On release, the inbox panel gets key back only if it was open when the handoff began.
- **Fade (spec §4.4).** `Sending`, `Done`, `Error` and `Speaking` fade 1.5 s after they are shown; spoken text uses D18. An outcome that arrives after a fade is shown again as `Done`.

Replace `app/src-tauri/src/voice/hud.rs`, keeping the tests from Step 1 at the bottom:

```rust
//! The voice HUD (voice spec §4.4): a second non-activating panel, top
//! centre of the active screen, 18% from the top. It takes key focus only
//! while it waits for input (confirm, which-one, countdown); see D17.
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, AtomicU64, Ordering};
use std::time::Duration;

use acd_voice::hud::{Hud, HudEvent};
use tauri::{AppHandle, Emitter, LogicalPosition, LogicalSize, Manager, Size, WebviewUrl, WebviewWindow};
use tauri_nspanel::{CollectionBehavior, ManagerExt, PanelBuilder, PanelHandle, PanelLevel, StyleMask, tauri_panel};

use crate::frame::Frame;

pub const LABEL: &str = "hud";
pub const SIZE: (f64, f64) = (560.0, 260.0);
/// Spec §4.4: the HUD fades 1.5 s after the step fires or speech ends.
pub const FADE_AFTER: Duration = Duration::from_millis(1500);

/// Intentional key handoff (D17): set only when `apply` makes the HUD key
/// for input; cleared by `release_key`, or when the HUD resigns key on its
/// own because the user clicked elsewhere.
static HANDOFF: AtomicBool = AtomicBool::new(false);
/// The inbox panel was open when the handoff began; it gets key back.
static INBOX_WAS_OPEN: AtomicBool = AtomicBool::new(false);
/// The last state shown; a fade runs only if nothing newer arrived.
static LAST_SEQ: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);

tauri_panel! {
    panel!(HudPanel {
        config: {
            can_become_key_window: true,
            can_become_main_window: false,
            is_floating_panel: true
        }
    })
    panel_event!(HudPanelEvents {
        window_did_resign_key(notification: &NSNotification) -> bool
    })
}

/// Main thread only; call once from `setup`, after `panel::create`.
pub fn create(app: &AppHandle) -> tauri::Result<()> {
    let panel = PanelBuilder::<_, HudPanel>::new(app, LABEL)
        .url(WebviewUrl::App("index.html".into()))
        .title("Voice")
        .size(Size::Logical(LogicalSize::new(SIZE.0, SIZE.1)))
        .resizable(false)
        // Above the inbox panel, which is Floating.
        .level(PanelLevel::ModalPanel)
        .with_window(|w| w.decorations(false))
        .add_style_mask(StyleMask::empty().nonactivating_panel())
        .collection_behavior(
            CollectionBehavior::new()
                .can_join_all_spaces()
                .full_screen_auxiliary(),
        )
        .hides_on_deactivate(false)
        .corner_radius(8.0)
        .build()?;
    let events = HudPanelEvents::new();
    events.window_did_resign_key(|_| {
        // Our own release clears HANDOFF first, so this fires with it set
        // only when the user moved focus elsewhere: end the handoff and do
        // not pull the inbox panel back to key later.
        if HANDOFF.swap(false, Ordering::SeqCst) {
            INBOX_WAS_OPEN.store(false, Ordering::SeqCst);
        }
        false
    });
    panel.set_event_handler(Some(events.as_ref()));
    panel.hide();
    Ok(())
}

/// Whether the HUD holds key on purpose; the inbox panel then must not
/// hide on resigning key.
pub fn holds_key() -> bool {
    HANDOFF.load(Ordering::SeqCst)
}

/// Shows state `seq` (D22). One main-thread closure presents the panel,
/// checks that it is on screen, and only then emits the state, so the
/// webview can never render, and acknowledge, a step whose panel failed
/// to appear. A failure inside the closure emits nothing; the step then
/// fails closed on the controller's display timeout. False if the closure
/// could not be dispatched at all. Any thread; never blocks.
pub fn present(app: &AppHandle, seq: u64, hud: &Hud) -> bool {
    LAST_SEQ.store(seq, Ordering::SeqCst);
    let (hidden, wants_key) = (matches!(hud, Hud::Hidden), hud.wants_key());
    let event = match serde_json::to_value(HudEvent { seq, hud }) {
        Ok(v) => v,
        Err(error) => {
            tracing::error!(%error, "could not serialize the HUD state");
            return false;
        }
    };
    let handle = app.clone();
    let dispatched = app.run_on_main_thread(move || {
        let presented = apply(&handle, hidden, wants_key);
        if !should_emit(hidden, presented) {
            tracing::error!("the HUD panel is not on screen; the step is not shown");
            return;
        }
        // Broadcast like `inbox://view`; only the HUD webview listens.
        if let Err(error) = handle.emit("voice://hud", event) {
            tracing::error!(%error, "could not emit the HUD state");
        }
    });
    if let Err(error) = dispatched {
        tracing::error!(%error, "could not dispatch the HUD update");
        return false;
    }
    if hud.fades() {
        let handle = app.clone();
        let after = fade_delay(hud);
        tauri::async_runtime::spawn(async move {
            tokio::time::sleep(after).await;
            if LAST_SEQ.load(Ordering::SeqCst) != seq {
                return;
            }
            let inner = handle.clone();
            if let Err(error) = handle.run_on_main_thread(move || {
                apply(&inner, true, false);
            }) {
                tracing::error!(%error, "could not dispatch the HUD fade");
            }
            if let Err(error) = handle.emit("voice://hud-clear", seq) {
                tracing::error!(%error, "could not emit the HUD fade");
            }
        });
    }
    true
}

/// D18: spoken text stays 1.5 s plus 50 ms per character, within 3–15 s.
pub fn fade_delay(hud: &Hud) -> Duration {
    match hud {
        Hud::Speaking { text } => Duration::from_millis(1500 + 50 * text.char_len() as u64)
            .clamp(Duration::from_secs(3), Duration::from_secs(15)),
        _ => FADE_AFTER,
    }
}

/// Top centre of `screen`, 18% from its top (design doc, floating overlays).
pub fn place_on(screen: Frame, size: (f64, f64)) -> Frame {
    Frame {
        x: screen.x + (screen.w - size.0) / 2.0,
        y: screen.y + screen.h * 0.18,
        w: size.0,
        h: size.1,
    }
}

/// A state is emitted only once its panel is on screen; hiding needs no
/// panel.
fn should_emit(hidden: bool, presented: bool) -> bool {
    hidden || presented
}

/// Main thread. True when the panel ended up as asked: hidden, or on
/// screen.
fn apply(app: &AppHandle, hidden: bool, wants_key: bool) -> bool {
    let Ok(panel) = app.get_webview_panel(LABEL) else {
        return false;
    };
    if hidden {
        release_key(app, &panel);
        panel.hide();
        return true;
    }
    if let Some(window) = app.get_webview_window(LABEL) {
        position(&window);
    }
    if wants_key {
        if !HANDOFF.swap(true, Ordering::SeqCst) {
            INBOX_WAS_OPEN.store(crate::panel::is_visible(), Ordering::SeqCst);
            panel.show_and_make_key();
        }
    } else {
        release_key(app, &panel);
        panel.order_front_regardless();
    }
    panel.is_visible()
}

/// Ends the handoff: key goes back to the inbox panel if it was open,
/// else to whatever macOS picks for the frontmost app.
fn release_key(app: &AppHandle, hud: &PanelHandle<tauri::Wry>) {
    if !HANDOFF.swap(false, Ordering::SeqCst) {
        return;
    }
    hud.resign_key_window();
    if INBOX_WAS_OPEN.swap(false, Ordering::SeqCst)
        && crate::panel::is_visible()
        && let Ok(inbox) = app.get_webview_panel("panel")
    {
        inbox.make_key_window();
    }
}

/// On the screen under the cursor, else the primary.
fn position(window: &WebviewWindow) {
    let (full, _) = crate::panel::screen_frames(window);
    let Some(screen) = full.get(crate::panel::fallback_screen(window, &full)).copied() else {
        return;
    };
    let f = place_on(screen, SIZE);
    if let Err(error) = window.set_position(LogicalPosition::new(f.x, f.y)) {
        tracing::warn!(%error, "could not position the HUD");
    }
}
```

```ts
  // Voice (spec §4.3): what voice may target, published on every change.
  // The epoch is new per mount, so a reloaded panel starts afresh.
  const published = { epoch: `${Date.now().toString(36)}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)}`, version: 0 };
  $effect(() => {
    published.version += 1;
    const presentation: Presentation = {
      epoch: published.epoch,
      version: published.version,
      selected: st.selected,
      visible: visible.map((c) => c.key),
      machine_filter: filter,
      done_collapsed: !doneOpen,
    };
    api.publishPresentation(presentation).catch(() => {});
  });

  onMount(() => {
    let off: (() => void) | undefined;
    let closed = false;
    api
      .onVoiceSelect((key) => {
        // A card hidden only by the machine filter can be named (spec §5.2): show it.
        if (!visible.some((c) => c.key === key)) machineFilter = null;
        st = { ...st, selected: key };
      })
      .then((un) => (closed ? un() : (off = un)), () => {});
    return () => {
      closed = true;
      off?.();
    };
  });
```

### Task 15: Frontend: the HUD

Depends on Task 14.

**Files:**
- Create: `app/src/lib/hud.ts`, `app/src/components/Hud.svelte`, `app/src/components/Hud.test.ts`
- Modify: `app/src/lib/ipc.ts`, `app/src/lib/fixtures.ts`
- Modify: `app/src/App.svelte` (the `hud` branch), `app/src/App.test.ts` (replace the Task 13 `hud` test)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - the events `voice://hud` (`HudEvent`) and `voice://hud-clear` (seq);
  - the commands `voice_shown`, `voice_confirm`, `voice_choose` and `voice_cancel` (Task 12);
  - the serde shapes pinned by `the_hud_json_shape_is_the_frontend_contract` (Task 8).
- Produces:
  - In `lib/hud.ts`: the types `Hud`, `HudEvent`, `ProposalView`, `TargetView`, `OutcomeView`, `Refusal`, `NotSentReason`, `Provenance` and `Level`; `refusalText`, `outcomeText`, `wantsKey` and `awaitingDisplay`.
  - New `Api` methods: `onHud(cb: (e: HudEvent) => void)`, `onHudClear(cb: (seq: number) => void)`, `voiceShown(proposalId: number)`, `voiceConfirm(proposalId: number)`, `voiceChoose(index: number)` and `voiceCancel()`.
  - `FakeApi` gains `hud?: (e: HudEvent) => void` and `hudClear?: (seq: number) => void`.

HUD behaviour (spec §4.4, D22):

- **Ordering and acknowledgement.**
  - An event whose `seq` is not newer than the last one is dropped.
  - After rendering a state that awaits display, the component calls `voiceShown(proposal_id)`. That is a confirm, or a countdown whose `action_id` is still `null`. It first checks, after the DOM flush, that the same `seq` and proposal are still on screen. A proposal replaced before the flush is never acknowledged, and the controller's timeout fails it closed.
  - A countdown waiting for its acknowledgement shows "Starting…" and no bar.
- **Keys** act only while the state waits for input: Enter confirms (`confirm` only, with its `proposal_id`), digits 1–9 choose (`which_one` only), and Esc cancels. Every input also has a button.
- **Layout.**
  - The proposal (target, question, rule, text) sits in a scrolling body. Each payload block also scrolls on its own, with a bounded height.
  - The buttons and the countdown bar sit outside the scrolling body, so Cancel is always reachable.
  - Payloads are shown whole, never as HTML.
- **Sending** reads "Sent. Waiting for the result." It fades like the other end states; a later outcome comes back as `Done`.
- Nothing is written to the console.
- **Look** (obsidian-cockpit, floating overlays):
  - glass `rgba(9, 13, 22, 0.88)` with `blur(24px)`;
  - the bevel `inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.12)`;
  - the cyan ring `0 0 0 1px rgba(6, 182, 212, 0.4)`;
  - radius `--r`;
  - a cyan 2 s bar pinned to the lower edge.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `app/src/components/Hud.test.ts`:

```ts
import { cleanup, render, screen } from "@testing-library/svelte";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { tick } from "svelte";
import { afterEach, expect, test, vi } from "vitest";
import Hud from "./Hud.svelte";
import { fakeApi, view } from "../lib/fixtures";
import type { InboxView } from "../lib/bindings/InboxView";
import type { Hud as HudState, ProposalView, TargetView } from "../lib/hud";

afterEach(() => {
  cleanup();
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
});

const target: TargetView = {
  card: "minos/a",
  machine: "minos",
  agent: "Claude",
  title: "Fix the build",
  request: "bash approval",
  off_screen: false,
};

const proposal = (o: Partial<ProposalView> = {}): ProposalView => ({
  action: "answer",
  target,
  option: "Yes, always",
  effect: "allow_always",
  rule: "Bash(npm test:*)",
  question: "npm test",
  text: null,
  provenance: "selected",
  level: "confirm",
  ...o,
});

let seq = 0;

async function show(state: HudState, v: InboxView = view()) {
  const api = fakeApi();
  render(Hud, { api, view: v });
  await tick();
  api.hud!({ seq: ++seq, hud: state });
  await tick();
  await tick();
  return api;
}

const others = (calls: unknown[][]) => calls.filter((c) => c[0] !== "voiceShown");

test("hidden shows nothing", () => {
  const { container } = render(Hud, { api: fakeApi(), view: view() });
  expect(container.querySelector(".hud")).toBeNull();
});

test("a rendered proposal is acknowledged with its id; other states are not", async () => {
  let api = await show({ state: "confirm", proposal: proposal(), proposal_id: 7 });
  expect(api.calls).toContainEqual(["voiceShown", 7]);
  cleanup();
  api = await show({ state: "countdown", proposal: proposal({ level: "countdown" }), proposal_id: 8, action_id: null });
  expect(api.calls).toContainEqual(["voiceShown", 8]);
  expect(screen.getByText(/Starting/)).toBeTruthy();
  cleanup();
  api = await show({ state: "countdown", proposal: proposal({ level: "countdown" }), proposal_id: 8, action_id: "act-1" });
  expect(api.calls.some((c) => c[0] === "voiceShown")).toBe(false);
});

test("a proposal replaced before the DOM flush is never acknowledged", async () => {
  const api = fakeApi();
  render(Hud, { api, view: view() });
  await tick();
  // Two events with no flush between them: only the second renders.
  api.hud!({ seq: ++seq, hud: { state: "confirm", proposal: proposal(), proposal_id: 20 } });
  api.hud!({ seq: ++seq, hud: { state: "confirm", proposal: proposal({ option: "Yes" }), proposal_id: 21 } });
  await tick();
  await tick();
  expect(api.calls.filter((c) => c[0] === "voiceShown")).toEqual([["voiceShown", 21]]);
  api.hud!({ seq: ++seq, hud: { state: "countdown", proposal: proposal({ level: "countdown" }), proposal_id: 22, action_id: null } });
  api.hud!({ seq: ++seq, hud: { state: "speaking", text: "Nothing needs you." } });
  await tick();
  await tick();
  expect(api.calls.filter((c) => c[0] === "voiceShown")).toEqual([["voiceShown", 21]]);
});

test("an older state arriving late is dropped", async () => {
  const api = fakeApi();
  render(Hud, { api, view: view() });
  await tick();
  api.hud!({ seq: 10, hud: { state: "speaking", text: "newer" } });
  api.hud!({ seq: 9, hud: { state: "speaking", text: "older" } });
  await tick();
  expect(screen.getByText("newer")).toBeTruthy();
  expect(screen.queryByText("older")).toBeNull();
  api.hudClear!(9);
  await tick();
  expect(screen.getByText("newer")).toBeTruthy();
  api.hudClear!(10);
  await tick();
  expect(screen.queryByText("newer")).toBeNull();
});

test("confirm shows target, action and the whole payload; Enter confirms its id, Esc cancels", async () => {
  const api = await show({ state: "confirm", proposal: proposal(), proposal_id: 3 });
  expect(screen.getByText("Fix the build")).toBeTruthy();
  expect(screen.getByText("@ minos")).toBeTruthy();
  expect(screen.getByText("Bash(npm test:*)")).toBeTruthy();
  expect(screen.getByText("npm test")).toBeTruthy();
  await userEvent.keyboard("{Enter}");
  await userEvent.keyboard("{Escape}");
  expect(others(api.calls)).toEqual([["voiceConfirm", 3], ["voiceCancel"]]);
});

test("confirm and cancel have buttons too", async () => {
  const api = await show({ state: "confirm", proposal: proposal(), proposal_id: 4 });
  await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /Confirm/ }));
  await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /Cancel/ }));
  expect(others(api.calls)).toEqual([["voiceConfirm", 4], ["voiceCancel"]]);
});

test("a long payload scrolls inside its block and the buttons stay outside it", async () => {
  const text = `${"run the tests and then report back\n".repeat(400)}END`;
  await show({
    state: "confirm",
    proposal: proposal({ action: "send", option: null, effect: null, rule: null, question: null, text }),
    proposal_id: 5,
  });
  const payload = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".payload")!;
  expect(payload.textContent).toBe(text);
  expect(payload.classList.contains("block")).toBe(true);
  const body = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".body")!;
  for (const name of [/Confirm/, /Cancel/]) {
    expect(body.contains(screen.getByRole("button", { name }))).toBe(false);
  }
});

test("a target off screen is marked", async () => {
  await show({ state: "confirm", proposal: proposal({ target: { ...target, off_screen: true } }), proposal_id: 6 });
  expect(screen.getByText("@ minos").classList.contains("off-screen")).toBe(true);
});

test("which-one lists numbered candidates; digits and clicks choose", async () => {
  const api = await show({
    state: "which_one",
    transcript: "approve",
    candidates: [target, { ...target, card: "minos/b", title: "Write docs" }],
  });
  await userEvent.keyboard("2");
  await userEvent.keyboard("9");
  await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /Fix the build/ }));
  expect(api.calls).toEqual([["voiceChoose", 1], ["voiceChoose", 0]]);
});

test("the countdown reads the inbox action and cancels on Esc, not Enter", async () => {
  const v = view({ actions: [{ id: "act-1", card_key: "minos/a", label: "Yes", detail: null, fires_at_ms: Date.now() + 1500 }] });
  const api = await show(
    { state: "countdown", proposal_id: 9, action_id: "act-1", proposal: proposal({ level: "countdown", option: "Yes", effect: "allow", rule: null }) },
    v,
  );
  expect(screen.getByText(/Sending in 1\.\ds/)).toBeTruthy();
  await userEvent.keyboard("{Enter}");
  await userEvent.keyboard("{Escape}");
  expect(api.calls).toEqual([["voiceCancel"]]);
});

test("states that do not wait for input ignore keys", async () => {
  const api = await show({ state: "thinking", transcript: "approve" });
  await userEvent.keyboard("{Escape}{Enter}1");
  expect(api.calls).toEqual([]);
});

test("after firing it says it is waiting for the result", async () => {
  await show({ state: "sending", proposal: proposal(), too_late_to_cancel: false });
  expect(screen.getByText("Sent. Waiting for the result.")).toBeTruthy();
});

test("errors are classified; only 'didn't understand' repeats the transcript", async () => {
  await show({ state: "error", refusal: "did_not_understand", transcript: "frobnicate the thing" });
  expect(screen.getByText("Didn't understand.")).toBeTruthy();
  expect(screen.getByText(/frobnicate the thing/)).toBeTruthy();
  cleanup();
  await show({ state: "error", refusal: "display_failed", transcript: null });
  expect(screen.getByText("The HUD could not show the step. Nothing was sent.")).toBeTruthy();
});

test("outcomes and spoken text are shown", async () => {
  await show({ state: "done", proposal: null, outcome: { kind: "not_sent", reason: "answered_elsewhere" } });
  expect(screen.getByText("Answered elsewhere.")).toBeTruthy();
  cleanup();
  await show({ state: "speaking", text: "2 need you. On minos: Claude, Fix: bash approval." });
  expect(screen.getByText(/2 need you/)).toBeTruthy();
});

test("voice text never reaches the console", async () => {
  const spies = (["log", "info", "warn", "error", "debug"] as const).map((m) => vi.spyOn(console, m));
  const api = await show({ state: "thinking", transcript: "SECRET-T" });
  api.voiceConfirm = async () => {
    throw new Error("SECRET-E");
  };
  api.voiceShown = async () => {
    throw new Error("SECRET-S");
  };
  api.hud!({ seq: ++seq, hud: { state: "confirm", proposal: proposal({ action: "send", text: "SECRET-P" }), proposal_id: 11 } });
  await tick();
  await userEvent.keyboard("{Enter}");
  for (const spy of spies) for (const call of spy.mock.calls) expect(JSON.stringify(call)).not.toContain("SECRET");
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: HUD types and text**

Create `app/src/lib/hud.ts`:

```ts
import type { Effect } from "./bindings/Effect";

// Mirrors acd_voice::hud and acd_voice::refusal (serde shapes pinned by
// the_hud_json_shape_is_the_frontend_contract).

export type Refusal =
  | "nothing_to_answer"
  | "nothing_to_read"
  | "no_target"
  | "no_such_target"
  | "no_such_option"
  | "ambiguous_option"
  | "read_only"
  | "offline"
  | "cannot_reply"
  | "subagent_hidden"
  | "out_of_view"
  | "filter_hidden_not_named"
  | "target_mismatch"
  | "bad_text"
  | "did_not_understand"
  | "model_unavailable"
  | "model_failed"
  | "snapshot_changed"
  | "already_counting"
  | "nothing_to_cancel"
  | "already_sent"
  | "not_permitted"
  | "display_failed";

export type NotSentReason =
  | "offline"
  | "not_supported"
  | "agent_gone"
  | "answered_elsewhere"
  | "changed"
  | "agent_busy"
  | "hidden";

export type Provenance = "named" | "selected" | "only_pending" | "inferred";
export type Level = "none" | "countdown" | "confirm";

export type TargetView = {
  card: string;
  machine: string;
  agent: string;
  title: string;
  request: string | null;
  off_screen: boolean;
};

export type ProposalView = {
  action: "answer" | "answer_text" | "send";
  target: TargetView;
  option: string | null;
  effect: Effect | null;
  rule: string | null;
  question: string | null;
  text: string | null;
  provenance: Provenance | null;
  level: Level;
};

export type OutcomeView =
  | { kind: "delivered" }
  | { kind: "rejected"; code: string }
  | { kind: "uncertain" }
  | { kind: "cancelled" }
  | { kind: "not_sent"; reason: NotSentReason }
  | { kind: "selected" };

export type Hud =
  | { state: "hidden" }
  | { state: "listening" }
  | { state: "thinking"; transcript: string }
  | { state: "confirm"; proposal: ProposalView; proposal_id: number }
  | { state: "which_one"; candidates: TargetView[]; transcript: string }
  | { state: "countdown"; proposal: ProposalView; proposal_id: number; action_id: string | null }
  | { state: "sending"; proposal: ProposalView; too_late_to_cancel: boolean }
  | { state: "done"; proposal: ProposalView | null; outcome: OutcomeView }
  | { state: "error"; refusal: Refusal; transcript: string | null }
  | { state: "speaking"; text: string };

export type HudEvent = { seq: number; hud: Hud };

/** Classified messages (spec §9): never a provider body or model text. */
export const refusalText: Record<Refusal, string> = {
  nothing_to_answer: "Nothing to answer.",
  nothing_to_read: "Nothing to read.",
  no_target: "Say which agent.",
  no_such_target: "No agent by that name can take this.",
  no_such_option: "That option is not there.",
  ambiguous_option: "Say the option number.",
  read_only: "This request can't be answered here.",
  offline: "The machine is offline.",
  cannot_reply: "This agent can't take a message now.",
  subagent_hidden: "Subagent threads are hidden.",
  out_of_view: "That agent is out of view. Only a message that names it can reach it.",
  filter_hidden_not_named: "That agent is filtered out. Say its machine.",
  target_mismatch: "Not sure which agent. Nothing was sent.",
  bad_text: "That text can't be sent.",
  did_not_understand: "Didn't understand.",
  model_unavailable: "Model unavailable. Simple commands still work.",
  model_failed: "The model did not answer. Simple commands still work.",
  snapshot_changed: "The request changed. Nothing was sent.",
  already_counting: "A countdown is already running for this card.",
  nothing_to_cancel: "Nothing to cancel.",
  already_sent: "Already sent. It can't be undone.",
  not_permitted: "Voice can't do that.",
  display_failed: "The HUD could not show the step. Nothing was sent.",
};

const notSentText: Record<NotSentReason, string> = {
  offline: "Offline. Nothing was sent.",
  not_supported: "Not supported for this agent.",
  agent_gone: "The agent is gone.",
  answered_elsewhere: "Answered elsewhere.",
  changed: "The request changed. Nothing was sent.",
  agent_busy: "The agent is busy. Nothing was sent.",
  hidden: "Hidden. Nothing was sent.",
};

export function outcomeText(o: OutcomeView): string {
  switch (o.kind) {
    case "delivered":
      return "Sent.";
    case "rejected":
      return `Not sent: ${o.code.replaceAll("_", " ")}.`;
    case "uncertain":
      return "Check the terminal.";
    case "cancelled":
      return "Cancelled. Nothing was sent.";
    case "not_sent":
      return notSentText[o.reason];
    case "selected":
      return "Selected.";
  }
}

/** The HUD has key focus only while it waits for input (spec §4.4). */
export const wantsKey = (h: Hud): boolean =>
  h.state === "confirm" || h.state === "which_one" || h.state === "countdown";

/** The proposal the HUD must acknowledge once it is on screen (D22). */
export function awaitingDisplay(h: Hud): number | null {
  if (h.state === "confirm") return h.proposal_id;
  if (h.state === "countdown" && h.action_id === null) return h.proposal_id;
  return null;
}
```
