- [ ] **Step 4: The HUD component**

Create `app/src/components/Hud.svelte`:

```svelte
<script lang="ts">
  import { onMount, tick } from "svelte";
  import type { InboxView } from "../lib/bindings/InboxView";
  import { awaitingDisplay, outcomeText, refusalText, wantsKey, type Hud, type ProposalView } from "../lib/hud";
  import type { Api } from "../lib/ipc";

  /** acd-client's undo window (spec §8): the bar's full width. */
  const WINDOW_MS = 2000;

  let { api, view = null }: { api: Api; view?: InboxView | null } = $props();

  let hud = $state<Hud>({ state: "hidden" });
  let now = $state(Date.now());
  // The last state shown; older events are dropped (they can arrive late).
  const seen = { seq: 0 };

  // Rejections are dropped silently: the console never sees voice text.
  const quiet = (p: Promise<unknown>) => void p.catch(() => {});

  onMount(() => {
    const offs: (() => void)[] = [];
    let closed = false;
    const keep = (un: () => void) => (closed ? un() : offs.push(un));
    api
      .onHud((e) => {
        if (e.seq <= seen.seq) return;
        seen.seq = e.seq;
        hud = e.hud;
        const id = awaitingDisplay(e.hud);
        if (id === null) return;
        // Acknowledge only after the DOM shows it, and only if it is still
        // the state on screen: two events before one flush must not
        // acknowledge a proposal that never rendered (D22).
        quiet(
          tick().then(() => {
            if (seen.seq === e.seq && awaitingDisplay(hud) === id) return api.voiceShown(id);
          }),
        );
      })
      .then(keep, () => {});
    api
      .onHudClear((seq) => {
        if (seq === seen.seq) hud = { state: "hidden" };
      })
      .then(keep, () => {});
    const timer = setInterval(() => (now = Date.now()), 100);
    return () => {
      closed = true;
      offs.forEach((un) => un());
      clearInterval(timer);
    };
  });

  const action = $derived.by(() => {
    if (hud.state !== "countdown" || hud.action_id === null) return null;
    // Copied out of `hud`: TypeScript does not keep the narrowing inside the callback.
    const id = hud.action_id;
    return view?.actions.find((a) => a.id === id) ?? null;
  });
  const remaining = $derived(action ? Math.max(0, action.fires_at_ms - now) : 0);
  const fraction = $derived(Math.min(1, remaining / WINDOW_MS));

  const confirm = (id: number) => quiet(api.voiceConfirm(id));
  const cancel = () => quiet(api.voiceCancel());
  const choose = (i: number) => quiet(api.voiceChoose(i));

  function verb(p: ProposalView): string {
    switch (p.action) {
      case "answer":
        return `Answer “${p.option ?? "?"}”`;
      case "answer_text":
        return "Type this answer";
      case "send":
        return "Send this message";
    }
  }

  function key(e: KeyboardEvent) {
    if (!wantsKey(hud) || e.isComposing || e.keyCode === 229 || e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey || e.altKey) return;
    if (e.key === "Escape") {
      e.preventDefault();
      cancel();
    } else if (e.key === "Enter" && hud.state === "confirm") {
      e.preventDefault();
      confirm(hud.proposal_id);
    } else if (hud.state === "which_one" && /^[1-9]$/.test(e.key)) {
      const i = Number(e.key) - 1;
      if (i < hud.candidates.length) {
        e.preventDefault();
        choose(i);
      }
    }
  }
</script>

<svelte:window onkeydown={key} />

{#if hud.state !== "hidden"}
  <main class="hud" data-state={hud.state} aria-live="polite">
    <div class="body">
      {#if hud.state === "listening"}
        <p class="status"><span class="mic" aria-hidden="true"></span>Listening…</p>
      {:else if hud.state === "thinking"}
        <p class="status"><span class="pulse" aria-hidden="true"></span>Thinking</p>
        <p class="said">“{hud.transcript}”</p>
      {:else if hud.state === "confirm" || hud.state === "countdown" || hud.state === "sending"}
        {@const p = hud.proposal}
        <header class="target">
          <span class="agent">{p.target.agent}</span>
          <span class="machine" class:off-screen={p.target.off_screen}>@ {p.target.machine}</span>
          <span class="title">{p.target.title}</span>
        </header>
        <p class="what">{verb(p)}</p>
        {#if p.question}<pre class="block">{p.question}</pre>{/if}
        {#if p.rule}<p class="label">Always allow, from now on:</p><pre class="block rule">{p.rule}</pre>{/if}
        {#if p.text}<pre class="block payload">{p.text}</pre>{/if}
        {#if hud.state === "sending"}
          <p class="status">{hud.too_late_to_cancel ? "Already sent. It can't be undone." : "Sent. Waiting for the result."}</p>
        {/if}
      {:else if hud.state === "which_one"}
        <p class="what">Which one?</p>
        <ol class="candidates">
          {#each hud.candidates as c, i (c.card)}
            <li>
              <button onclick={() => choose(i)}>
                <kbd>{i + 1}</kbd>
                <span class="agent">{c.agent}</span>
                <span class="machine" class:off-screen={c.off_screen}>@ {c.machine}</span>
                <span class="title">{c.title}</span>
                {#if c.request}<span class="tag">{c.request}</span>{/if}
              </button>
            </li>
          {/each}
        </ol>
      {:else if hud.state === "done"}
        <p class="status" data-outcome={hud.outcome.kind}>{outcomeText(hud.outcome)}</p>
      {:else if hud.state === "error"}
        <p class="status error">{refusalText[hud.refusal]}</p>
        {#if hud.transcript}<p class="said">“{hud.transcript}”</p>{/if}
      {:else if hud.state === "speaking"}
        <p class="speech">{hud.text}</p>
      {/if}
    </div>
    {#if hud.state === "confirm"}
      {@const id = hud.proposal_id}
      <div class="buttons">
        <button class="primary" onclick={() => confirm(id)}>Confirm <kbd>⏎</kbd></button>
        <button onclick={cancel}>Cancel <kbd>Esc</kbd></button>
      </div>
    {:else if hud.state === "countdown"}
      <div class="buttons">
        <button class="undo" onclick={cancel}>
          {#if hud.action_id === null}Starting…{:else}Sending in {(remaining / 1000).toFixed(1)}s{/if} · <kbd>Esc</kbd> to cancel
        </button>
      </div>
      {#if hud.action_id !== null}
        <span class="track" aria-hidden="true"><span class="bar" style:width="{fraction * 100}%"></span></span>
      {/if}
    {:else if hud.state === "which_one"}
      <div class="buttons"><button onclick={cancel}>Cancel <kbd>Esc</kbd></button></div>
    {/if}
  </main>
{/if}

<style>

      inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.12),
      0 0 0 1px rgba(6, 182, 212, 0.4);
  }
  /* Scrolls; the buttons below never do, so Cancel stays reachable. */
  .body {
    display: flex;

    overflow-y: auto;
  }

  }
  .agent {
    font-weight: 600;

    font: 500 11px var(--font-mono);

  }

  .block {
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Manual check on the bundle (macOS)**

Build and install the app (`just app-install`, or `cd app && npx tauri build --bundles app` and open the bundle). Record what you see; tests do not cover window focus.

1. **Settings.** Turn on "Voice control" and "Show the typed command field". The inbox panel shows the "Voice command" field. Turning voice off hides the field and the HUD.
2. **Approve.** With one pending card selected, type `approve` and press Enter.
   - The HUD appears top centre, 18% down, above the inbox panel.
   - It reads "Starting…", then shows the 2 s bar.
   - The answer goes out. The HUD shows "Sent. Waiting for the result.", then "Sent.", and fades.
   - The frontmost app never changes.
3. **Confirm.** Type `yes always`.
   - The HUD takes key and shows the whole rule; the inbox panel stays open.
   - Enter confirms.
   - After the countdown the inbox panel has key again, and J/K work.
4. **Cancel.** Type `approve`, then press Esc in the HUD during the countdown: nothing is sent. Repeat, and type `cancel` instead: also nothing.
5. **Selection.** With two pending cards, move the selection to the second with J and type `approve`: only the highlighted card is answered. Move the selection to a Working card and type `approve`: the HUD says "Nothing to answer." and nothing is sent.
6. **Other phrases.** `next` and `previous` move the selection. `read it` shows "Bash approval. …" and fades after a few seconds. `tell box claude to continue` gives "Model unavailable. Simple commands still work." (D14).
7. **Focus handoff (D17).**
   - a. **Inbox click.** While the HUD shows Confirm, click a card in the inbox panel. Expect: the HUD stops holding key, the inbox panel stays open, and clicking Confirm in the HUD still works.
   - b. **Other app.** While the HUD shows Confirm, click into another app. Expect: the HUD stops holding key, the inbox panel stays open but not key, and the other app keeps focus after the step ends.
   - c. **Hotkey.** While the HUD shows Confirm, press the inbox hotkey. Expect: the panel toggles as usual, the HUD stays, and Esc in the HUD still cancels.
   - d. **Spaces.** Switch Spaces while the HUD waits. Expect: the HUD follows (it joins all Spaces) and nothing is sent.
   - e. **Full screen.** With a full-screen app in front, run step 2. Expect: the HUD shows over it (full-screen auxiliary) and the app stays in front.
8. **Silent HUD (D22).** Quit and relaunch, then type `approve` within the first second, before the HUD webview has loaded. Expect "The HUD could not show the step. Nothing was sent.", or a normal countdown if it loaded in time; never a send without the HUD.

## Spec coverage

| Spec | Where |
|---|---|
| §1 "nothing is sent unless the HUD showed the complete target, action and payload" | D22: Tasks 8, 11, 13 and 15 |
| §4.1 modules `snapshot`, `fast`, `router`, `safety`, `plan`, `speech` | Tasks 1–8. `hud`, `text` and `refusal` are extra modules for the HUD view model and redaction. |
| §4.3 presentation snapshot, frozen with generation, agent id, request id, revision, option effects | Task 1 (types), Task 9 (`StampedView`), Task 10 (freeze), Task 14 (publish) |
| §4.4 HUD: second panel, non-activating, key only while waiting, Enter/digits/Esc and buttons, trigger cancels, 1.5 s fade, above the inbox panel | Tasks 13 and 15; focus is checked by hand (Task 15 Step 6) |
| §5.1 steps | Task 6 (`Step`), Task 8 (execution) |
| §5.2 provenance and visibility, including D7 | Tasks 1 and 3 (and 6, 10, 11 for D7) |
| §5.3 fast path; spoken "confirm" (§6.1) and "the first" (§5.2 rule 5) | Task 2 (`parse_reply`), Task 8 (`Executor::reply`), Task 11 |
| §5.4 model contract, caps, schema | Task 5 |
| §5.5 untrusted text, validation | Tasks 5 and 6 |
| §5.6 executor, outcome stream, `cancel(action_id)`, recheck of card, agent, request id and revision, generation | Tasks 8, 9, 10 and 11 |
| §6.1 confirmation table | Task 4 |
| §6.3 no voice content in logs, `Debug`, errors or the console; cleared when voice is turned off | Tasks 1, 5, 6, 8, 9, 11 and 15 (tests); `Controller::set_enabled` (Task 11) |
| §7 templated speech | Task 7; shown as text in Task 15 |
| §8 Voice settings (step-1 subset) | Tasks 12 and 14 |
| §9 classified errors, "Didn't understand" with the transcript, "Model unavailable", snapshot changed | Tasks 8 and 15 |
| §10 tests listed for step 1 | Tasks 2–11 and 15 |

Not in this plan, by design (spec §11 steps 2–5): the trigger, capture, transcriber and speaker traits and backends; the 60 s limit and the microphone indicator; permissions and signing; Foundation Models and the cloud model; TTS; firing alerts; multi-step plans; backend B; the HUD utterance history (D20).
